/* ============================================================
 * 文件：app.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 词灵岛 · 四六级卡通英语学习小程序
 * 应用入口：初始化本地学习数据，记录设备信息，维护每日学习状态。
 */
const store = require('./utils/store.js')

App({
  globalData: {
    appName: '词灵岛',
    version: '1.0.0',
    systemInfo: null,
    statusBarHeight: 20,
    // 页面间临时传递的学习结果
    lastSession: null
  },

  onLaunch() {
    // 初始化本地状态（含版本迁移与默认值补齐）
    store.init()

    try {
      const info = wx.getSystemInfoSync()
      this.globalData.systemInfo = info
      this.globalData.statusBarHeight = info.statusBarHeight || 20
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('获取系统信息失败', e)
    }

    // 刷新宠物心情与每日任务
    store.touchOpen()
    store.ensureDaily()
  },

  onShow() {
    store.ensureDaily()
  },

  onError(err) {
    console.error('[词灵岛] 运行异常：', err)
  }
})

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：components/cartoon-card/index.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 卡通卡片容器：统一的描边、硬阴影与胶带装饰
 */
Component({
  options: {
    multipleSlots: true,
    addGlobalClass: true

})

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：components/pet-avatar/index.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 原创伙伴形象：5 个物种 × 5 段形态，纯 CSS 绘制
 * species: sprout / ink / whale / turtle / fox
 * stage: 0-4
 */
const ACC_MAP = {
  hat: '🎩',
  scarf: '🧣',
  crown: '👑',
  wings: '✨',
  none: ''
}

Component({
  options: {
    addGlobalClass: true
  },
  properties: {
    size: { type: Number, value: 220 },
    species: { type: String, value: 'sprout' },
    color: { type: String, value: '#FFD166' },
    accent: { type: String, value: '#6FD39B' },
    accessory: { type: String, value: 'none' },
    mood: { type: Number, value: 80 },
    stage: { type: Number, value: 0 },
    branch: { type: String, value: '' },
    animate: { type: Boolean, value: true }
  },
  data: {
    scale: 1,
    happy: true,
    accEmoji: ''
  },
  observers: {
    size: function (val) {
      const s = Number(val) || 220
      this.setData({ scale: s / 220 })
    },
    mood: function (val) {
      this.setData({ happy: Number(val) >= 40 })
    },
    accessory: function (val) {
      this.setData({ accEmoji: ACC_MAP[val] || '' })
    }
  },
  lifetimes: {
    attached() {
      this.setData({
        scale: (Number(this.data.size) || 220) / 220,
        happy: Number(this.data.mood) >= 40,
        accEmoji: ACC_MAP[this.data.accessory] || ''
      })
    }
  }
})

<template name="face">
  <view class="face face-{{species}}">
    <view class="eye eye-l"><view class="glint"></view></view>
    <view class="eye eye-r"><view class="glint"></view></view>
    <view class="blush blush-l"></view>
    <view class="blush blush-r"></view>
    <view class="mouth {{happy ? 'is-happy' : 'is-sad'}}"></view>
  </view>
</template>

<view class="pet-wrap" style="width:{{size}}rpx;height:{{size}}rpx;">
  <view class="pet-scale" style="transform:scale({{scale}});">
    <view
      class="pet species-{{species}} stage-{{stage}} branch-{{branch}} {{animate ? 'an
im-float' : ''}}"
      style="--pc:{{color}};--pa:{{accent}};"
    >
      <view class="pet-shadow"></view>

      <!-- 芽芽：叶片灵体 -->
      <block wx:if="{{species === 'sprout'}}">
        <view class="sprout-stem"></view>
        <view class="sprout-leaf sprout-leaf-l"></view>
        <view class="sprout-leaf sprout-leaf-r"></view>
        <view class="sprout-body body"></view>
        <view class="sprout-foot sprout-foot-l"></view>
        <view class="sprout-foot sprout-foot-r"></view>
        <view wx:if="{{stage >= 2}}" class="sprout-spark">✦</view>
      </block>

      <!-- 乌乌：墨团小猫 -->
      <block wx:elif="{{species === 'ink'}}">
        <view class="ink-tail"></view>
        <view class="ink-ear ink-ear-l"></view>
        <view class="ink-ear ink-ear-r"></view>
        <view class="ink-body body"></view>
        <view class="ink-drip ink-drip-1"></view>
        <view class="ink-drip ink-drip-2"></view>
        <view wx:if="{{stage >= 1}}" class="ink-horn ink-horn-l"></view>
        <view wx:if="{{stage >= 1}}" class="ink-horn ink-horn-r"></view>
        <view wx:if="{{stage >= 3}}" class="ink-aura"></view>
      </block>

      <!-- 泡泡：云鲸 -->
      <block wx:elif="{{species === 'whale'}}">
        <view class="whale-spout"><view class="spout-drop"></view></view>
        <view class="whale-tail"></view>
        <view class="whale-fin whale-fin-l"></view>
        <view class="whale-fin whale-fin-r"></view>
        <view class="whale-body body"></view>
        <view class="whale-belly"></view>
        <view wx:if="{{stage >= 2}}" class="whale-star">✦</view>
        <view wx:if="{{stage >= 4}}" class="whale-ring"></view>
      </block>

      <!-- 咔哒：齿轮龟 -->
      <block wx:elif="{{species === 'turtle'}}">
        <view class="turtle-leg turtle-leg-1"></view>
        <view class="turtle-leg turtle-leg-2"></view>
        <view class="turtle-leg turtle-leg-3"></view>
        <view class="turtle-leg turtle-leg-4"></view>
        <view class="turtle-tail"></view>
        <view class="turtle-shell"></view>
        <view class="turtle-gear"><view class="gear-core"></view></view>
        <view class="turtle-head"></view>
        <view wx:if="{{stage >= 2}}" class="turtle-antenna"><view class="antenna-dot">
</view></view>
      </block>

      <!-- 阿燎：焰狐 -->
      <block wx:else>
        <view class="fox-tail fox-tail-1"></view>
        <view wx:if="{{stage >= 2}}" class="fox-tail fox-tail-2"></view>
        <view wx:if="{{stage >= 4}}" class="fox-tail fox-tail-3"></view>
        <view class="fox-ear fox-ear-l"></view>
        <view class="fox-ear fox-ear-r"></view>
        <view class="fox-body body"></view>
        <view class="fox-muzzle"></view>
        <view wx:if="{{stage >= 3}}" class="fox-ember">✦</view>
      </block>

      <template is="face" data="{{species: species, happy: happy}}" />
      <view wx:if="{{branch}}" class="branch-mark">{{branch === 'a' ? '◆' : '✧'}}</vie
w>
      <text wx:if="{{accEmoji}}" class="pet-acc">{{accEmoji}}</text>
    </view>
  </view>
</view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：components/ring-progress/index.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 环形进度：使用 conic-gradient 绘制，无需 canvas
 */
Component({
  options: {
    addGlobalClass: true
  },
  properties: {
    percent: { type: Number, value: 0 },
    size: { type: Number, value: 160 },
    color: { type: String, value: '#FF9F1C' },
    value: { type: String, value: '' },
    label: { type: String, value: '' },
    track: { type: String, value: '#F1E7D8' }
  },
  data: {
    ringStyle: '',
    innerSize: 0
  },
  observers: {
    'percent, size, color, track': function () {
      this.refresh()
    }
  },
  lifetimes: {
    attached() {
      this.refresh()
    }
  },
  methods: {
    refresh() {
      const p = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(this.data.percent) || 0))
      const size = Number(this.data.size) || 160
      const deg = Math.round(p * 3.6)
      this.setData({
        ringStyle:
          'width:' + size + 'rpx;height:' + size + 'rpx;background:conic-gradient(' +
          this.data.color + ' ' + deg + 'deg,' + this.data.track + ' ' + deg + 'deg);'
,
        innerSize: size - 26
      })
    }
  }
})

    <text wx:if="{{label}}" class="ring-label">{{label}}</text>
    <slot></slot>
  </view>
</view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：custom-tab-bar/index.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 自定义底部导航：悬浮胶囊式卡通 TabBar
 * 各 Tab 页面在 onShow 中调用 getTabBar().setSelected(index) 同步高亮。
 */
Component({
  data: {
    selected: 0,
    list: [
      { pagePath: 'pages/index/index', text: '小岛', icon: '🏝️', color: '#FF9F1C' },
      { pagePath: 'pages/books/books', text: '词书', icon: '📖', color: '#2EC4B6' },
      { pagePath: 'pages/pet/pet', text: '小窝', icon: '🐣', color: '#FF86AE' },
      { pagePath: 'pages/profile/profile', text: '我的', icon: '🎒', color: '#9B7EDE' 
}
    ]
  },
  methods: {
    setSelected(index) {
      if (this.data.selected !== index) {
        this.setData({ selected: index })
      }
    },
    onTap(e) {
      const index = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
      const item = this.data.list[index]
      if (!item) return
      if (index === this.data.selected) return
      wx.switchTab({
        url: '/' + item.pagePath,
        success: () => {
          this.setData({ selected: index })
        }
      })
    }
  }
})


/* ============================================================
 * 文件：custom-tab-bar/index.json
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
{
  "component": true
}


/* ============================================================
 * 文件：custom-tab-bar/index.wxml
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
<view class="tabbar">
  <view
    wx:for="{{list}}"
    wx:key="pagePath"
    class="tab-item {{selected === index ? 'is-active' : ''}}"
    data-index="{{index}}"
    bindtap="onTap"
  >
    <view class="tab-icon-wrap" style="{{selected === index ? 'background:' + item.col
or : ''}}">
      <text class="tab-icon">{{item.icon}}</text>
      <view wx:if="{{selected === index}}" class="tab-dot"></view>
    </view>
    <text class="tab-text">{{item.text}}</text>
  </view>
  <view class="safe-area"></view>
</view>

/**

const byId = {}
PETS.forEach((p) => {
  byId[p.id] = p
})

function getById(id) {
  return byId[id] || PETS[0]
}

module.exports = {
  list: PETS,
  getById: getById
}

cet4.forEach((w) => {
  w.book = 'cet4'
})
cet6.forEach((w) => {
  w.book = 'cet6'
})

/* ============================================================
 * 全量词库合并
 * cet4-full.js / cet6-full.js 为开源词库生成的紧凑数组，
 * 结构：[单词, 音标, 中文释义, 词性]。
 * 精选词（含例句、词根、记忆提示）优先保留，全量词只补充词义。
 * 同一单词只归属一本词书，避免统计重复。
 * ============================================================ */
const fullCet4 = require('./cet4-full.js')
const fullCet6 = require('./cet6-full.js')
const usedWords = {}

cet4.forEach((w) => {
  usedWords[w.word.toLowerCase()] = true
})
cet6.forEach((w) => {
  usedWords[w.word.toLowerCase()] = true
})

function appendFullWords(list, rows, book) {
  let added = 0
  rows.forEach((row, index) => {
    const word = row[0]
    if (usedWords[word]) return
    usedWords[word] = true
    list.push({
      id: book + '-f' + index,
      word: word,
      phonetic: row[1],
      cn: row[2],
      pos: row[3] || '',
      en: '',
      cnSent: '',
      roots: [],
      tip: '',
      tag: book === 'cet4' ? '四级' : '六级',
      book: book
    })
    added += 1
  })
  return added
}

appendFullWords(cet4, fullCet4, 'cet4')
appendFullWords(cet6, fullCet6, 'cet6')

function byWord(a, b) {
  const x = a.word.toLowerCase()
  const y = b.word.toLowerCase()
  return x < y ? -1 : x > y ? 1 : 0
}
cet4.sort(byWord)
cet6.sort(byWord)

const all = cet4.concat(cet6)
const byId = {}
all.forEach((w) => {
  byId[w.id] = w
})

const BOOKS = [
  {
    id: 'cet4',
    name: '四级全量词',
    short: 'CET-4',
    emoji: '🐣',
    color: 'orange',
    desc: '大学英语四级全量核心词汇',
    total: cet4.length
  },
  {
    id: 'cet6',
    name: '六级全量词',
    short: 'CET-6',
    emoji: '🦊',
    color: 'purple',
    desc: '大学英语六级全量进阶词汇',
    total: cet6.length
  }
]

function getById(id) {
  return byId[id] || null
}

function getBook(id) {
  return id === 'cet6' ? cet6 : cet4
}

module.exports = {
  cet4: cet4,
  cet6: cet6,
  all: all,
  books: BOOKS,
  getById: getById,
  getBook: getBook
}

    })
  },

    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
  },

    return {
      title: '词灵岛 · 让四六级单词长成一座会发光的小岛',
      path: '/pages/index/index'
    }
  }
})

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/achievements/achievements.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 成就墙
 * 展示全部成就的解锁状态与完成进度。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')

Page({
  data: {
    list: [],
    view: [],
    filter: 'all',
    unlockedCount: 0,
    total: 0,
    percent: 0,
    filters: [
      { key: 'all', label: '全部' },
      { key: 'unlocked', label: '已解锁' },
      { key: 'locked', label: '未解锁' }
    ]
  },

  onShow() {
    this.refresh()
  },

  refresh() {
    const list = store.getAchievements()
    const unlockedCount = list.filter((a) => a.unlocked).length
    this.setData({
      list: list,
      unlockedCount: unlockedCount,
      total: list.length,
      percent: Math.round((unlockedCount / list.length) * 100)
    })
    this.applyFilter(this.data.filter)
  },

  applyFilter(filter) {
    const list = this.data.list.filter((a) => {
      if (filter === 'unlocked') return a.unlocked
      if (filter === 'locked') return !a.unlocked
      return true
    })
    this.setData({ filter: filter, view: list })
  },

  onFilter(e) {
    this.applyFilter(e.currentTarget.dataset.key)
  },

  goStudy() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/study/study' })
  }
})

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/books/books.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 词书页 · 选择词书、查看掌握情况、进入学习/复习/拼图
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const audio = require('../../utils/audio.js')
const wordsData = require('../../data/words.js')

const PAGE_SIZE = 60

Page({
  data: {
    books: wordsData.books,
    bookId: 'cet4',
    book: null,
    progress: { total: 0, learned: 0, mastered: 0, due: 0, percent: 0 },
    filtered: [],
    filteredTotal: 0,
    hasMore: false,
    filter: 'all',
    keyword: '',
    filters: [
      { key: 'all', label: '全部' },
      { key: 'new', label: '未学' },
      { key: 'learning', label: '学习中' },
      { key: 'mastered', label: '已掌握' }
    ],
    detail: null,
    showDetail: false,
    showPhonetic: true,
    detailWordSize: 52
  },

  onLoad(options) {
    const bookId = options && options.book === 'cet6' ? 'cet6' : 'cet4'
    this.setData({ bookId: bookId })
    this.refresh()
  },

  onShow() {
    if (typeof this.getTabBar === 'function' && this.getTabBar()) {
      this.getTabBar().setSelected(1)
    }
    this.refresh()
  },

  onUnload() {
    if (this.searchTimer) clearTimeout(this.searchTimer)
  },

  onReachBottom() {
    this.loadMore()
  },

  refresh() {
    const bookId = this.data.bookId
    const book = wordsData.books.filter((b) => b.id === bookId)[0] || wordsData.books[
0]
    const raw = wordsData.getBook(bookId)
    const progress = store.getBookProgress(raw, bookId)
    this.allWords = raw.map((w) => {
      const r = store.getRecord(w.id)
      const level = r ? r.level : 0
      return {
        id: w.id,
        word: w.word,
        phonetic: w.phonetic,
        cn: w.cn,
        pos: w.pos,
        level: level,
        statusText: !r ? '未学' : level >= 4 ? '已掌握' : '学习中',
        statusClass: !r ? 'tag' : level >= 4 ? 'tag-green' : 'tag-orange'
      }
    })
    this.setData({
      book: book,
      progress: progress,
      showPhonetic: store.getState().settings.showPhonetic
    })
    this.setFiltered(this.applyFilter(this.allWords, this.data.filter, this.data.keywo
rd), true)
  },

  /** 仅向视图层投递一页数据，避免 3000+ 词条一次性渲染 */
  setFiltered(full, reset) {
    this.filteredFull = full
    let visible
    if (reset) {
      visible = full.slice(0, PAGE_SIZE)
    } else {
      const start = this.data.filtered.length
      visible = this.data.filtered.concat(full.slice(start, start + PAGE_SIZE))
    }
    this.setData({
      filtered: visible,
      filteredTotal: full.length,
      hasMore: visible.length < full.length
    })
  },

  loadMore() {
    if (!this.data.hasMore) return
    this.setFiltered(this.filteredFull, false)
  },

  applyFilter(list, filter, keyword) {
    const kw = (keyword || '').trim().toLowerCase()
    return list.filter((w) => {
      let ok = true
      if (filter === 'new') ok = w.level === 0 && w.statusText === '未学'
      else if (filter === 'learning') ok = w.statusText === '学习中'
      else if (filter === 'mastered') ok = w.statusText === '已掌握'
      if (!ok) return false
      if (!kw) return true
      return w.word.toLowerCase().indexOf(kw) >= 0 || w.cn.indexOf(kw) >= 0
    })
  },

  switchBook(e) {
    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id
    if (id === this.data.bookId) return
    this.setData({ bookId: id, keyword: '' })
    this.refresh()
  },

  onFilter(e) {
    const key = e.currentTarget.dataset.key
    this.setData({ filter: key })
    this.setFiltered(this.applyFilter(this.allWords, key, this.data.keyword), true)
  },

  onSearch(e) {
    const kw = e.detail.value
    this.setData({ keyword: kw })
    if (this.searchTimer) clearTimeout(this.searchTimer)
    this.searchTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.setFiltered(this.applyFilter(this.allWords, this.data.filter, kw), true)
    }, 180)
  },

  clearSearch() {
    if (this.searchTimer) clearTimeout(this.searchTimer)
    this.setData({ keyword: '' })
    this.setFiltered(this.applyFilter(this.allWords, this.data.filter, ''), true)
  },

  openWord(e) {
    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id
    const word = wordsData.getById(id)
    if (!word) return
    const record = store.getRecord(id)
    const detail = Object.assign({}, word, {
      seen: record ? record.seen : 0,
      right: record ? record.right : 0,
      level: record ? record.level : 0,
      levelText: util.levelText(record ? record.level : 0)
    })
    const length = Math.max(4, word.word.length)
    this.setData({
      detail: detail,
      detailWordSize: Math.max(26, Math.min(52, Math.floor(560 / length))),
      showDetail: true
    })
  },

  closeDetail() {
    this.setData({ showDetail: false })
  },

  speak() {
    if (!this.data.detail) return
    if (!this.data.detail.phonetic) {
      wx.showToast({ title: '暂无音标', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    audio.playWord(this.data.detail.word)
    wx.showToast({
      title: this.data.detail.phonetic,
      icon: 'none',
      duration: 1200
    })
  },

  startStudy() {
    const remain = this.allWords.filter((w) => w.statusText === '未学').length
    if (remain === 0) {
      wx.showToast({ title: '这本词书的新词已学完，去复习吧', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/study/study?book=' + this.data.bookId })
  },

  startReview() {
    const due = store.getDueWords(wordsData.getBook(this.data.bookId), 40)
    if (!due.length) {
      wx.showToast({ title: '暂时没有到期单词，先学点新词吧', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/review/review?book=' + this.data.bookId })
  },

  startPuzzle() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/puzzle/puzzle' })
  },

  onShareAppMessage() {
    return {
      title: '词灵岛四六级词书：' + (this.data.book ? this.data.book.name : ''),
      path: '/pages/books/books'
    }
  },

  noop() {}
})

/* ============================================================

 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
<view class="page page-tab">
  <!-- 词书切换 -->
  <view class="book-tabs">
    <view
      wx:for="{{books}}"
      wx:key="id"
      class="book-tab {{bookId === item.id ? 'is-active' : ''}}"
      data-id="{{item.id}}"
      bindtap="switchBook"
    >
      <text class="book-emoji">{{item.emoji}}</text>
      <view class="flex1">
        <text class="book-name">{{item.name}}</text>
        <text class="book-short">{{item.short}} · {{item.total}} 词</text>
      </view>
      <view wx:if="{{bookId === item.id}}" class="book-check">✓</view>
    </view>
  </view>

  <!-- 学习进度 -->
  <view class="summary card card-{{book.color}}">
    <ring-progress percent="{{progress.percent}}" size="150" color="#FF9F1C" value="{{
progress.percent}}%" label="已学习" />
    <view class="summary-info">
      <view class="summary-title">{{book.name}}</view>
      <view class="summary-desc">{{book.desc}}</view>
      <view class="summary-stats">
        <text class="summary-stat">已学 {{progress.learned}}</text>
        <text class="summary-stat">掌握 {{progress.mastered}}</text>
        <text class="summary-stat">待复习 {{progress.due}}</text>
      </view>
    </view>
  </view>

  <!-- 三种进入方式 -->
  <view class="mode-row">
    <view class="mode mode-new press" bindtap="startStudy">
      <text class="mode-icon">🌱</text>
      <text class="mode-name">学新词</text>
      <text class="mode-desc">每日推进</text>
    </view>
    <view class="mode mode-review press" bindtap="startReview">
      <text class="mode-icon">🔁</text>
      <text class="mode-name">智能复习</text>
      <text class="mode-desc">{{progress.due}} 词到期</text>
    </view>
    <view class="mode mode-puzzle press" bindtap="startPuzzle">
      <text class="mode-icon">🧩</text>
      <text class="mode-name">词根拼图</text>
      <text class="mode-desc">拆词闯关</text>
    </view>
  </view>

  <!-- 搜索 -->
  <view class="search-row">
    <view class="search-box">
      <text class="search-icon">🔍</text>
      <input
        class="search-input"
        placeholder="搜索单词或中文释义"
        placeholder-class="placeholder"
        value="{{keyword}}"
        bindinput="onSearch"
        confirm-type="search"
      />
      <text wx:if="{{keyword}}" class="search-clear" bindtap="clearSearch">✕</text>
    </view>
  </view>

  <!-- 筛选 -->
  <scroll-view scroll-x class="filter-scroll" enhanced show-scrollbar="{{false}}">
    <view class="filter-row">
      <view
        wx:for="{{filters}}"
        wx:key="key"
        class="filter-chip {{filter === item.key ? 'is-active' : ''}}"
        data-key="{{item.key}}"
        bindtap="onFilter"
      >{{item.label}}</view>
    </view>
  </scroll-view>

  <!-- 单词列表 -->
  <view class="word-list">
    <view
      wx:for="{{filtered}}"
      wx:key="id"
      class="word-row press"
      data-id="{{item.id}}"
      bindtap="openWord"
    >
      <view class="word-main">
        <view class="row gap-12">
          <text class="word-text">{{item.word}}</text>
          <text class="tiny" wx:if="{{showPhonetic}}">{{item.phonetic}}</text>
        </view>
        <text class="word-cn">{{item.cn}}</text>
      </view>
      <text class="tag {{item.statusClass}}">{{item.statusText}}</text>
    </view>
    <empty-state
      wx:if="{{filtered.length === 0}}"
      emoji="🔎"
      title="没有找到匹配的单词"
      desc="换个关键词，或切换筛选条件试试"
    />
  </view>

  <view class="list-count">共 {{filteredTotal}} 个单词 · 已显示 {{filtered.length}} 个
</view>
  <view wx:if="{{hasMore}}" class="load-more" bindtap="loadMore">加载更多 ↓</view>

  <!-- 单词详情 -->
  <view wx:if="{{showDetail}}" class="mask" bindtap="closeDetail">
    <view class="modal detail-modal" catchtap="noop">
      <view class="detail-hd">
        <view class="flex1">
          <text class="detail-word" style="font-size:{{detailWordSize}}rpx;">{{detail.
word}}</text>
          <view class="detail-phonetic" wx:if="{{detail.phonetic}}">{{detail.phonetic}
}</view>
        </view>
        <view class="detail-speak" bindtap="speak">🔊</view>
      </view>
      <view class="detail-cn">
        <text wx:if="{{detail.pos}}" class="detail-pos">{{detail.pos}}</text>
        <text>{{detail.cn}}</text>
      </view>
      <view class="detail-level">
        <text class="tag tag-orange">{{detail.levelText}}</text>
        <text class="tag">学习 {{detail.seen}} 次</text>
        <text class="tag tag-green">答对 {{detail.right}} 次</text>
      </view>
      <view class="detail-block" wx:if="{{detail.en}}">
        <text class="detail-label">例句</text>
        <text class="detail-en">{{detail.en}}</text>
        <text class="detail-cn-sent">{{detail.cnSent}}</text>
      </view>
      <view class="detail-block" wx:if="{{detail.roots.length}}">
        <text class="detail-label">词根拆解</text>
        <view class="root-row">
          <view wx:for="{{detail.roots}}" wx:key="t" class="root-chip">
            <text class="root-text">{{item.t}}</text>
            <text class="root-mean">{{item.m}}</text>
          </view>
        </view>
      </view>
      <view class="detail-tip" wx:if="{{detail.tip}}">💡 {{detail.tip}}</view>
      <view class="detail-empty" wx:if="{{!detail.en}}">
        该词暂无例句，先记住词义与发音；在阅读和听力里再遇见它时，印象会更深。
      </view>
      <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block mt-24" bindtap="closeDetail">知道啦</view
>
    </view>
  </view>
</view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/chapters/chapters.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 剧情闯关 · 词灵岛主线
 * 累计词汇量达到章节门槛后解锁，阅读剧情并完成章节可获得小鱼干。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')

Page({
  data: {
    chapters: [],
    totalLearned: 0,
    finishedCount: 0,
    active: null,
    showStory: false
  },

  onShow() {
    this.refresh()
  },

  refresh() {
    const state = store.getState()
    const chapters = store.getChapters()
    this.setData({
      chapters: chapters,
      totalLearned: state.stats.totalLearned,
      finishedCount: chapters.filter((c) => c.finished).length
    })
  },

  openChapter(e) {
    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id
    const chapter = this.data.chapters.filter((c) => c.id === id)[0]
    if (!chapter) return
    if (!chapter.unlocked) {
      wx.showToast({
        title: '再学 ' + (chapter.need - this.data.totalLearned) + ' 个单词解锁',
        icon: 'none'
      })
      return
    }
    this.setData({ active: chapter, showStory: true })
  },

  closeStory() {
    this.setData({ showStory: false })
  },

  complete() {
    if (!this.data.active) return
    store.finishChapter(this.data.active.id)
    wx.showToast({ title: '章节完成，小鱼干 +25', icon: 'none' })
    this.setData({ showStory: false })
    this.refresh()
  },

  goStudy() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/study/study' })
  },

  noop() {}
})

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/dialogue/dialogue.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 情景对话 · 角色扮演
 * NPC 逐句发问，学习者在三个回应中选择最得体的一项，
 * 系统根据回应质量累计得分，结束后给出评级与关键表达。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const dialogues = require('../../data/dialogues.js')

Page({
  data: {
    scenes: dialogues.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, title: d.title, emoji: d.emoji })),
    activeId: 'd1',
    scene: null,
    turns: [],
    turnIndex: 0,
    transcript: [],
    options: [],
    chosen: -1,
    answered: false,
    feedback: null,
    score: 0,
    maxScore: 0,
    phase: 'chat',
    scrollTo: '',
    result: null
  },

  onLoad(options) {
    const id = options && options.id ? options.id : 'd1'
    this.loadScene(id)
  },

  loadScene(id) {
    const scene = dialogues.filter((d) => d.id === id)[0] || dialogues[0]
    const turns = scene.turns.map((t) => {
      const copy = Object.assign({}, t)
      copy.options = util.shuffle(t.options).map((o, i) => Object.assign({}, o, { lett
er: 'ABC'[i] }))
      return copy
    })
    const first = turns[0]
    const transcript = [
      { id: 'msg_0', role: 'npc', en: first.npc.en, cn: first.npc.cn }
    ]
    this.setData({
      activeId: scene.id,
      scene: scene,
      turns: turns,
      turnIndex: 0,
      transcript: transcript,
      options: first.options,
      chosen: -1,
      answered: false,
      feedback: null,
      score: 0,
      maxScore: turns.length * 10,
      phase: 'chat',
      result: null,
      scrollTo: 'msg_0'
    })
  },

  selectScene(e) {
    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id
    if (id === this.data.activeId) return
    this.loadScene(id)
  },

  choose(e) {
    if (this.data.answered) return
    const idx = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    const option = this.data.options[idx]
    if (!option) return
    const transcript = this.data.transcript.slice()
    transcript.push({
      id: 'msg_' + transcript.length,
      role: 'me',
      en: option.en,
      cn: option.cn
    })
    const score = this.data.score + option.score
    this.setData({
      chosen: idx,
      answered: true,
      feedback: option,
      score: score,
      transcript: transcript,
      scrollTo: 'msg_' + (transcript.length - 1)
    })
    if (option.score >= 10) store.vibrate('light')
  },

  nextTurn() {
    const next = this.data.turnIndex + 1
    if (next >= this.data.turns.length) {
      this.finish()
      return
    }
    const turn = this.data.turns[next]
    const transcript = this.data.transcript.slice()
    transcript.push({
      id: 'msg_' + transcript.length,
      role: 'npc',
      en: turn.npc.en,
      cn: turn.npc.cn
    })
    this.setData({
      turnIndex: next,
      transcript: transcript,
      options: turn.options,
      chosen: -1,
      answered: false,
      feedback: null,
      scrollTo: 'msg_' + (transcript.length - 1)
    })
  },

  finish() {
    const rate = this.data.maxScore ? this.data.score / this.data.maxScore : 0
    const stars = rate >= 0.9 ? 3 : rate >= 0.65 ? 2 : 1
    const comment =
      rate >= 0.9
        ? '表达自然又得体，像在当地生活了很久。'
        : rate >= 0.65
        ? '基本能完成交流，注意礼貌用语会更地道。'
        : '先把关键句型记住，再回来挑战一次吧。'
    const correct = Math.round(this.data.score / 10)
    store.logActivity({
      exp: 20 + correct * 4,
      fish: 10,
      correct: correct,
      wrong: Math.max(0, this.data.turns.length - correct),
      minutes: 3,
      quizzes: this.data.turns.length
    })
    store.markFinished('dialogues', this.data.scene.id)
    this.setData({
      phase: 'done',
      result: {
        score: this.data.score,
        max: this.data.maxScore,
        stars: stars,
        comment: comment,
        rate: Math.round(rate * 100)
      }
    })
  },

  restart() {
    this.loadScene(this.data.activeId)
  },

  goHome() {
    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
  },

  speakLine(e) {
    const index = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    const line = this.data.transcript[index]
    if (!line) return
    wx.showToast({ title: line.en, icon: 'none', duration: 1600 })
  }
})

<view class="page dialogue-page">
  <!-- 场景选择 -->
  <scroll-view scroll-x class="scene-scroll" enhanced show-scrollbar="{{false}}">
    <view class="scene-row">
      <view
        wx:for="{{scenes}}"
        wx:key="id"
        class="scene-chip {{activeId === item.id ? 'is-active' : ''}}"
        data-id="{{item.id}}"
        bindtap="selectScene"
      >
        <text class="scene-chip-emoji">{{item.emoji}}</text>
        <text>{{item.title}}</text>
      </view>
    </view>
  </scroll-view>

  <block wx:if="{{phase === 'chat'}}">
    <!-- 场景信息 -->
    <view class="scene-card">
      <view class="scene-emoji">{{scene.emoji}}</view>
      <view class="flex1">
        <view class="scene-title">{{scene.title}}</view>
        <view class="scene-sub">{{scene.cnTitle}} · 第 {{turnIndex + 1}}/{{turns.lengt
h}} 轮</view>
      </view>
      <view class="scene-score">⭐ {{score}}/{{maxScore}}</view>
    </view>

    <!-- 对话窗口 -->
    <scroll-view
      scroll-y
      class="chat"
      scroll-into-view="{{scrollTo}}"
      scroll-with-animation
      enhanced
      show-scrollbar="{{false}}"
    >
      <view class="chat-inner">
        <view
          wx:for="{{transcript}}"
          wx:key="id"
          id="{{item.id}}"
          class="msg {{item.role === 'me' ? 'msg-me' : ''}}"
        >
          <view class="avatar {{item.role === 'me' ? 'avatar-me' : ''}}">{{item.role =
== 'me' ? '🐣' : '🧑‍🏫'}}</view>
          <view class="bubble-wrap">
            <view class="bubble {{item.role === 'me' ? 'bubble-me' : ''}}" data-index=
"{{index}}" bindtap="speakLine">
              <text class="bubble-en">{{item.en}}</text>
              <text class="bubble-cn">{{item.cn}}</text>
            </view>
            <text class="bubble-tip">{{item.role === 'me' ? '你' : '对方'}} · 点击可看
全文</text>
          </view>
        </view>
        <view class="chat-pad"></view>
      </view>
    </scroll-view>

    <!-- 回应选择 -->
    <view wx:if="{{!answered}}" class="reply-panel">
      <view class="reply-title">选择一个最合适的回应</view>
      <view
        wx:for="{{options}}"
        wx:key="en"
        class="reply-option"
        data-index="{{index}}"
        bindtap="choose"
      >
        <view class="reply-letter">{{item.letter}}</view>
        <view class="flex1">
          <text class="reply-en">{{item.en}}</text>
          <text class="reply-cn">{{item.cn}}</text>
        </view>
      </view>
    </view>

    <!-- 反馈 -->
    <view wx:else class="feedback">
      <view class="feedback-hd">
        <text class="feedback-emoji">{{feedback.score >= 10 ? '🌟' : feedback.score >=
 5 ? '👍' : '🤔'}}</text>
        <text class="feedback-title">{{feedback.score >= 10 ? '很地道！' : feedback.sc
ore >= 5 ? '可以，但还能更好' : '换个说法会更礼貌'}}</text>
        <text class="feedback-score">+{{feedback.score}}</text>
      </view>
      <text class="feedback-tip">💡 {{turns[turnIndex].tip}}</text>
      <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block" bindtap="nextTurn">
        {{turnIndex + 1 >= turns.length ? '查看本次表现' : '继续对话'}}
      </view>
    </view>
  </block>

  <!-- 结算 -->
  <block wx:else>
    <view class="done-card">
      <view class="sticker sticker-pink">对话完成</view>
      <view class="done-stars">
        <text wx:for="{{[1,2,3]}}" wx:key="*this" class="star {{index < result.stars ?
 'is-on' : ''}}">★</text>
      </view>
      <view class="done-score">{{result.score}}<text class="done-unit">/{{result.max}}
</text></view>
      <view class="done-label">表达得分</view>
      <view class="done-comment">{{result.comment}}</view>
      <view class="phrase-box">
        <view class="phrase-title">本场关键表达</view>
        <view class="phrase-row">
          <text wx:for="{{scene.keyPhrases}}" wx:key="*this" class="phrase">{{item}}</
text>
        </view>
      </view>
      <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block" bindtap="restart">再演一次</view>
      <view class="btn btn-ghost btn-block mt-16" bindtap="goHome">回到小岛</view>
    </view>
  </block>
</view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/exam/exam.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 模拟考场
 * 20 道词汇题、15 分钟倒计时、答题卡跳转，交卷后按 710 分制估分。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const wordsData = require('../../data/words.js')

const EXAM_TOTAL = 20
const EXAM_SECONDS = 15 * 60
const PASS_LINE = 425

function formatTime(sec) {
  const s = Math.max(0, sec)
  const m = Math.floor(s / 60)
  const r = s % 60
  return (m < 10 ? '0' + m : m) + ':' + (r < 10 ? '0' + r : r)
}

Page({
  data: {
    phase: 'intro',
    history: [],
    total: EXAM_TOTAL,
    seconds: EXAM_SECONDS,
    timeText: '15:00',
    index: 0,
    questions: [],
    answers: [],
    selected: -1,
    answeredCount: 0,
    showSheet: false,
    result: null,
    passLine: PASS_LINE,
    lastScore: 0
  },

  onLoad() {
    this.refreshHistory()
  },

  onShow() {
    if (this.data.phase === 'exam' && !this.timer) {
      this.startTimer()
    }
  },

  onUnload() {
    this.stopTimer()
  },

  onHide() {
    this.stopTimer()
  },

  refreshHistory() {
    const state = store.getState()
    const history = state.exam.history.slice(0, 5).map((e, index) => ({
      id: String(e.at) + '_' + index,
      date: util.formatDate(e.at, 'MM-DD HH:mm'),
      score: e.score,
      correct: e.correct,
      total: e.total,
      pass: e.score >= PASS_LINE
    }))
    this.setData({
      history: history,
      lastScore: history.length ? history[0].score : 0
    })
  },

  start() {
    const pool = util.shuffle(wordsData.all).slice(0, EXAM_TOTAL)
    const questions = pool.map((word) => {
      const built = util.buildOptions(word, wordsData.all, 4)
      return {
        id: word.id,
        word: word,
        options: built.options.map((o, i) => ({ text: o.text, letter: 'ABCD'[i] })),
        answer: built.answer,
        size: Math.max(28, Math.min(68, Math.floor(600 / Math.max(4, word.word.length)
)))
      }
    })
    const answers = []
    for (let i = 0; i < questions.length; i++) answers.push(-1)
    this.setData({
      phase: 'exam',
      questions: questions,
      answers: answers,
      index: 0,
      qIndex: 0,
      selected: -1,
      answeredCount: 0,
      timeLeft: EXAM_SECONDS,
      timeText: formatTime(EXAM_SECONDS),
      showSheet: false,
      result: null
    })
    this.startTimer()
  },

  startTimer() {
    this.stopTimer()
    this.timer = setInterval(() => {
      const left = this.data.timeLeft - 1
      if (left <= 0) {
        this.setData({ timeLeft: 0, timeText: '00:00' })
        this.stopTimer()
        wx.showToast({ title: '时间到，自动交卷', icon: 'none' })
        this.finish()
        return
      }
      this.setData({ timeLeft: left, timeText: formatTime(left) })
    }, 1000)
  },

  stopTimer() {
    if (this.timer) {
      clearInterval(this.timer)
      this.timer = null
    }
  },

  choose(e) {
    const idx = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    const answers = this.data.answers.slice()
    answers[this.data.index] = idx
    let count = 0
    answers.forEach((a) => {
      if (a >= 0) count += 1
    })
    this.setData({
      answers: answers,
      selected: idx,
      answeredCount: count
    })
  },

  prev() {
    if (this.data.index <= 0) return
    const index = this.data.index - 1
    this.setData({ index: index, qIndex: index, selected: this.data.answers[index] })
  },

  next() {
    if (this.data.index >= this.data.total - 1) return
    const index = this.data.index + 1
    this.setData({ index: index, qIndex: index, selected: this.data.answers[index] })
  },

  toggleSheet() {
    this.setData({ showSheet: !this.data.showSheet })
  },

  jump(e) {
    const index = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    this.setData({
      index: index,
      qIndex: index,
      selected: this.data.answers[index],
      showSheet: false
    })
  },

  confirmSubmit() {
    const unanswered = this.data.total - this.data.answeredCount
    if (unanswered > 0) {
      wx.showModal({
        title: '还有 ' + unanswered + ' 题未作答',
        content: '确定现在交卷吗？',
        confirmText: '交卷',
        success: (res) => {
          if (res.confirm) this.finish()
        }
      })
      return
    }
    this.finish()
  },

  finish() {
    if (this.data.phase !== 'exam') return
    this.stopTimer()
    const questions = this.data.questions
    const answers = this.data.answers
    let correct = 0
    const wrongList = []
    questions.forEach((q, i) => {
      if (answers[i] === q.answer) {
        correct += 1
      } else {
        wrongList.push({
          word: q.word.word,
          cn: q.word.cn,
          chosen: answers[i] >= 0 ? q.options[answers[i]].text : '未作答',
          answer: q.options[q.answer].text
        })
        store.addWrong(q.word.id, '遗忘', { word: q.word.word, cn: q.word.cn })
      }
    })
    const score = Math.round((correct / questions.length) * 710)
    const minutes = Math.round((EXAM_SECONDS - this.data.timeLeft) / 60)
    store.saveExamResult({
      score: score,
      correct: correct,
      wrong: questions.length - correct,
      total: questions.length,
      minutes: minutes
    })
    this.setData({
      phase: 'done',
      result: {
        score: score,
        correct: correct,
        total: questions.length,
        wrong: questions.length - correct,
        accuracy: Math.round((correct / questions.length) * 100),
        minutes: minutes,
        pass: score >= PASS_LINE,
        wrongList: wrongList,
        comment:
          score >= 500
            ? '状态很不错，保持这个节奏去考场。'
            : score >= PASS_LINE
            ? '已经过线，继续巩固错题会更稳。'
            : '先把错词过一遍，再来一次会进步很快。'
      }
    })
    this.refreshHistory()
  },

  restart() {
    this.start()
  },

  goHome() {
    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
  },

  goWrongbook() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/wrongbook/wrongbook' })
  },

  goStats() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/stats/stats' })
  },

  noop() {}

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/garden/garden.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 知识花园
 * 每掌握一个单词就种下一朵花，花园规模与装饰随学习进度生长。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')

const DECOR_EMOJI = {
  sun: '☀️',
  butterfly: '🦋',
  fence: '🪵',
  lantern: '🏮',
  fountain: '⛲',
  rainbow: '🌈'
}

const DECOR_NAME = {
  sun: '暖阳',
  butterfly: '小蝴蝶',
  fence: '木栅栏',
  lantern: '暖光灯',
  fountain: '许愿喷泉',
  rainbow: '彩虹拱门'
}

Page({
  data: {
    plots: [],
    cells: [],
    count: 0,
    decorList: [],
    nextDecor: null,
    nextPercent: 0,
    stats: null,
    detail: null,
    showDetail: false
  },

  onShow() {
    this.refresh()
  },

  refresh() {
    const view = store.getGardenView()
    const cells = view.plots.map((p) => Object.assign({}, p, { empty: false }))
    const min = Math.max(12, Math.ceil((view.plots.length + 1) / 4) * 4)
    const total = Math.min(36, min)
    for (let i = cells.length; i < total; i++) {
      cells.push({ id: 'empty_' + i, empty: true })
    }
    const decorList = view.decor.map((id) => ({
      id: id,
      emoji: DECOR_EMOJI[id] || '✨',
      name: DECOR_NAME[id] || '装饰'
    }))
    const next = view.nextDecor
    this.setData({
      plots: view.plots,
      cells: cells,
      count: view.count,
      decorList: decorList,
      nextDecor: next,
      nextPercent: next ? Math.min(100, Math.round((view.count / next.need) * 100)) : 
100,
      stats: store.getStatsView()
    })
  },

  openPlot(e) {
    const index = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    const plot = this.data.plots[index]
    if (!plot) return
    this.setData({
      detail: Object.assign({}, plot, {
        dateText: util.formatDate(plot.plantedAt, 'YYYY年MM月DD日')
      }),
      showDetail: true
    })
  },

  closeDetail() {
    this.setData({ showDetail: false })
  },

  goStudy() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/study/study' })
  },

  goPet() {
    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/pet/pet' })
  },

  noop() {}
})

 * 文件：pages/garden/garden.json
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0

  "navigationBarTitleText": "知识花园"

 * 文件：pages/garden/garden.wxml
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
<view class="page garden-page">
  <view class="garden-hd">
    <view>
      <view class="h1">知识花园</view>
      <view class="sub">每掌握一个单词，就有一朵花盛开</view>
    </view>
    <view class="garden-count">
      <text class="garden-count-num">{{count}}</text>
      <text class="garden-count-label">朵花</text>
    </view>
  </view>

  <!-- 花园场景 -->
  <view class="garden-scene">
    <view class="sun"></view>
    <view class="cloud c1"></view>
    <view class="cloud c2"></view>

    <view wx:if="{{decorList[1]}}" class="decor decor-butterfly">🦋</view>
    <view wx:if="{{decorList[2]}}" class="decor decor-fence">🪵</view>
    <view wx:if="{{decorList[3]}}" class="decor decor-lantern">🏮</view>
    <view wx:if="{{decorList[4]}}" class="decor decor-fountain">⛲</view>
    <view wx:if="{{decorList[5]}}" class="decor decor-rainbow">🌈</view>

    <view class="garden-grid">
      <view
        wx:for="{{cells}}"
        wx:key="id"
        class="plot {{item.empty ? 'is-empty' : ''}}"
        data-index="{{index}}"
        bindtap="openPlot"
      >
        <block wx:if="{{!item.empty}}">
          <view class="flower">
            <view class="flower-head" style="--fc:{{item.color}};">
              <view class="petal p1"></view>
              <view class="petal p2"></view>
              <view class="petal p3"></view>
              <view class="petal p4"></view>
              <view class="petal p5"></view>
              <view class="flower-core"></view>
            </view>
            <view class="flower-stem">
              <view class="flower-leaf"></view>
            </view>
          </view>
          <text class="plot-word">{{item.word}}</text>
        </block>
        <text wx:else class="plot-plus">+</text>
      </view>
    </view>

    <view class="ground"></view>
  </view>

  <!-- 花园数据 -->
  <view class="garden-stats">
    <view class="gstat">
      <text class="num">{{count}}</text>
      <text class="gstat-label">盛开的花朵</text>
    </view>
    <view class="gstat-divider"></view>
    <view class="gstat">
      <text class="num">{{decorList.length}}</text>
      <text class="gstat-label">已解锁装饰</text>
    </view>
    <view class="gstat-divider"></view>
    <view class="gstat">
      <text class="num">{{stats.streak}}</text>
      <text class="gstat-label">连续打卡</text>
    </view>
  </view>

  <!-- 下一装饰 -->
  <view wx:if="{{nextDecor}}" class="next-card">
    <view class="next-hd">
      <text class="next-title">🎁 下一件花园装饰</text>
      <text class="next-name">{{nextDecor.name}}</text>
    </view>
    <view class="bar bar-thin bar-green">
      <view class="bar-inner" style="width:{{nextPercent}}%"></view>
    </view>
    <text class="next-tip">再掌握 {{nextDecor.need - count}} 个单词解锁（{{count}}/{{n
extDecor.need}}）</text>
  </view>
  <view wx:else class="next-card is-done">
    <text class="next-title">🏆 花园装饰已全部解锁</text>
    <text class="next-tip">这座花园已经开满啦，继续学习会种出更多花。</text>
  </view>

  <!-- 装饰墙 -->
  <view class="decor-card">
    <view class="decor-title">装饰图鉴</view>
    <view class="decor-row">
      <view wx:for="{{decorList}}" wx:key="id" class="decor-item">
        <text class="decor-emoji">{{item.emoji}}</text>
        <text class="decor-name">{{item.name}}</text>
      </view>
      <view wx:if="{{decorList.length === 0}}" class="decor-empty">还没有装饰，先去种
花吧</view>
    </view>
  </view>

  <view class="garden-tip">
    <text class="garden-tip-title">🌱 花园小秘密</text>
    <text class="garden-tip-text">单词熟练度达到 4 级（连续答对并跨过复习间隔）才会开
花。已经开花的单词，即使后来答错也不会凋谢，只会暂时合拢花瓣。</text>
  </view>

  <view class="garden-actions">
    <view class="btn btn-primary flex1" bindtap="goStudy">去种一朵花</view>
    <view class="btn btn-ghost flex1" bindtap="goPet">看看词灵</view>
  </view>

  <!-- 花朵详情 -->
  <view wx:if="{{showDetail}}" class="mask" bindtap="closeDetail">
    <view class="modal flower-modal" catchtap="noop">
      <view class="flower-big">
        <view class="flower-head big" style="--fc:{{detail.color}};">
          <view class="petal p1"></view>
          <view class="petal p2"></view>
          <view class="petal p3"></view>
          <view class="petal p4"></view>
          <view class="petal p5"></view>
          <view class="flower-core"></view>
        </view>
      </view>
      <view class="modal-title">{{detail.word}}</view>
      <view class="modal-desc">{{detail.cn}}</view>
      <view class="flower-meta">种植于 {{detail.dateText}}</view>
      <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block mt-24" bindtap="closeDetail">继续逛花园</
view>
    </view>
  </view>
</view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/index/index.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 首页 · 学习岛
 * 汇总今日任务、词灵状态、剧情进度与八个特色玩法入口。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const quotes = require('../../data/quotes.js')
const wordsData = require('../../data/words.js')

const FEATURES = [
  { icon: '🧩', name: '词根拼图', desc: '拆词记忆', url: '/pages/puzzle/puzzle', color
: 'purple' },
  { icon: '🎧', name: '影子跟读', desc: '听说训练', url: '/pages/listening/listening',
 color: 'blue' },
  { icon: '📖', name: '阅读闯关', desc: '真题精读', url: '/pages/reading/reading', col
or: 'green' },
  { icon: '💬', name: '情景对话', desc: '角色扮演', url: '/pages/dialogue/dialogue', c
olor: 'pink' },
  { icon: '✍️', name: '写作工坊', desc: '即时诊断', url: '/pages/writing/writing', co
lor: 'orange' },
  { icon: '🩺', name: '错题病历', desc: '对症下药', url: '/pages/wrongbook/wrongbook',
 color: 'red' },
  { icon: '🌷', name: '知识花园', desc: '单词开花', url: '/pages/garden/garden', color
: 'green' },
  { icon: '⏱️', name: '模拟考场', desc: '全真计时', url: '/pages/exam/exam', color: '
blue' }
]

Page({
  data: {
    greeting: '',
    dateText: '',
    nickname: '',
    avatar: '',
    fish: 0,
    streak: 0,
    daysLeft: 0,
    examDateText: '',
    pet: null,
    done: 0,
    target: 30,
    todayPercent: 0,
    dueCount: 0,
    tasks: [],
    tasksDone: 0,
    features: FEATURES,
    chapter: null,
    quote: null,
    stats: null,
    accuracy: 0
  },

  onLoad() {
    this.refresh()
  },

  onShow() {
    if (typeof this.getTabBar === 'function' && this.getTabBar()) {
      this.getTabBar().setSelected(0)
    }
    this.refresh()
  },

  refresh() {
    const state = store.getState()
    const today = store.getToday()
    const pet = store.getPetView()
    const tasks = store.getTasks()
    const stats = store.getStatsView()
    const chapters = store.getChapters()
    const chapter = chapters.filter((c) => !c.finished)[0] || chapters[chapters.length
 - 1]
    const profile = state.profile
    const target = Math.max(10, profile.dailyNew + profile.dailyReview)
    const done = today.newWords + today.reviews
    const due = store.getBookProgress(wordsData.all, 'all').due
    const accuracy = stats.accuracy

    this.setData({
      greeting: util.greeting(),
      dateText: util.formatDate(new Date(), 'MM月DD日') + ' · ' + util.weekdayCN(new D
ate()),
      nickname: profile.nickname,
      avatar: profile.avatar,
      fish: state.wallet.fish,
      streak: state.stats.streak,
      daysLeft: util.daysUntil(profile.examDate),
      examDateText: profile.examDate,
      pet: pet,
      done: done,
      target: target,
      todayPercent: util.percent(done, target),
      dueCount: due,
      tasks: tasks,
      tasksDone: tasks.filter((t) => t.done).length,
      chapter: chapter,
      quote: util.dailyQuote(quotes),
      stats: stats,
      accuracy: accuracy
    })
  },

  goStudy() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/study/study' })
  },

  goReview() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/review/review' })
  },

  goUrl(e) {
    const url = e.currentTarget.dataset.url
    if (url) wx.navigateTo({ url: url })
  },

  goPet() {
    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/pet/pet' })
  },

  goChapters() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/chapters/chapters' })
  },

  goPlan() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/plan/plan' })
  },

  onClaim(e) {
    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id
    const res = store.claimTask(id)
    if (res.ok) {
      wx.showToast({ title: res.msg, icon: 'none' })
      store.vibrate('light')
      this.refresh()
    } else {
      wx.showToast({ title: res.msg, icon: 'none' })
    }
  },

  onShareAppMessage() {
    return {
      title: '我在词灵岛养了一群吃单词的小精灵，一起来打卡四六级！',
      path: '/pages/index/index'
    }
  }
})

    <text class="quote-mark">“</text>
    <text class="quote-en">{{quote.en}}</text>
    <text class="quote-cn">{{quote.cn}}</text>
    <text class="quote-author">—— {{quote.author}}</text>
    <view class="quote-plan" bindtap="goPlan">制定备考计划 →</view>
  </view>

  <view class="footer-tip">词灵岛 · 让每个单词都长成花园里的花</view>
</view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/listening/listening.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 影子跟读 · 听说训练
 * 1. 选择材料，模拟播放并显示波形进度；
 * 2. 逐句跟读，调用录音接口采集时长并给出示例评分；
 * 3. 完成听力理解题，汇总本次成绩。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const listenings = require('../../data/listenings.js')

Page({
  data: {
    materials: listenings,
    activeId: 'l1',
    material: null,
    phase: 'listen',
    playing: false,
    playingIndex: -1,
    playProgress: 0,
    playTimeText: '00:00',
    totalTimeText: '00:00',
    showScript: true,
    sentenceScores: {},
    shadowAvg: 0,
    shadowCount: 0,
    recording: false,
    recIndex: -1,
    recSeconds: 0,
    quizIndex: 0,
    quizSelected: -1,
    quizAnswered: false,
    quizCorrect: 0,
    quizTotal: 0,
    quizQuestion: null,
    result: null
  },

  onLoad(options) {
    const id = options && options.id ? options.id : 'l1'
    this.initRecorder()
    this.selectMaterialById(id)
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
    this._active = true
  },

  onUnload() {
    this._active = false
    this.stopTimers()
    this.stopRecordSilently()
    if (!this.finished) {
      const minutes = (Date.now() - (this.startedAt || Date.now())) / 60000
      if (minutes >= 0.5) store.addMinutes(minutes)
    }
  },

  onHide() {
    this.stopTimers()
    this.setData({ playing: false })
  },

  initRecorder() {
    try {
      this.recorder = wx.getRecorderManager()
      this.recorder.onStop(() => this.handleRecordStop())
      this.recorder.onError(() => {
        this.setData({ recording: false, recIndex: -1 })
        wx.showToast({ title: '录音失败，请检查麦克风权限', icon: 'none' })
      })
    } catch (e) {
      this.recorder = null
    }
  },

  selectMaterialById(id) {
    const material = listenings.filter((m) => m.id === id)[0] || listenings[0]
    const totalWords = material.sentences.reduce((n, s) => n + s.en.split(/\s+/).lengt
h, 0)
    const seconds = Math.max(12, Math.round((totalWords / 2.4) * 10) / 10)
    this.totalSeconds = seconds
    this.setData({
      activeId: material.id,
      material: material,
      phase: 'listen',
      playing: false,
      playingIndex: -1,
      playProgress: 0,
      playTimeText: '00:00',
      totalTimeText: util.formatDate(new Date(seconds * 1000), 'mm:ss'),
      sentenceScores: {},
      shadowAvg: 0,
      shadowCount: 0,
      recording: false,
      recIndex: -1,
      quizIndex: 0,
      quizCorrect: 0,
      quizAnswered: false,
      quizSelected: -1
    })
    this.stopTimers()
  },

  selectMaterial(e) {
    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id
    if (id === this.data.activeId) return
    this.selectMaterialById(id)
  },

  toggleScript() {
    this.setData({ showScript: !this.data.showScript })
  },

  /** 模拟整段播放（示例工程用于演示流程与波形动画） */
  togglePlay() {
    if (this.data.playing) {
      this.pausePlay()
      return
    }
    this.playStart = Date.now()
    this.playBase = (this.data.playProgress / 100) * this.totalSeconds
    this.setData({ playing: true })
    this.playTimer = setInterval(() => {
      const elapsed = (Date.now() - this.playStart) / 1000 + this.playBase
      const percent = Math.min(100, Math.round((elapsed / this.totalSeconds) * 100))
      const shown = Math.min(this.totalSeconds, elapsed)
      this.setData({
        playProgress: percent,
        playTimeText: util.formatDate(new Date(shown * 1000), 'mm:ss')
      })
      if (percent >= 100) {
        this.pausePlay()
        this.setData({ playProgress: 100, playTimeText: this.data.totalTimeText })
      }
    }, 200)
  },

  pausePlay() {
    if (this.playTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.playTimer)
      this.playTimer = null
    }
    this.setData({ playing: false })
  },

  playSentence(e) {
    const index = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    const sentence = this.data.material.sentences[index]
    if (!sentence) return
    if (this.playSentenceTimer) clearTimeout(this.playSentenceTimer)
    this.setData({ playingIndex: index })
    const ms = Math.max(1200, sentence.en.split(/\s+/).length * 420)
    this.playSentenceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.setData({ playingIndex: -1 })
    }, ms)
    wx.showToast({ title: '正在示范第 ' + (index + 1) + ' 句', icon: 'none', duration:
 700 })
  },

  /* ---------------- 录音跟读 ---------------- */
  startShadow(e) {
    if (this.data.recording) {
      this.stopRecord()
      return
    }
    const index = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    if (!this.recorder) {
      wx.showToast({ title: '当前环境不支持录音', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    const self = this
    this.ensureRecordAuth(() => {
      self.beginRecord(index)
    })
  },

  ensureRecordAuth(cb) {
    wx.getSetting({
      success: (res) => {
        const auth = res.authSetting || {}
        if (auth['scope.record'] === false) {
          wx.showModal({
            title: '需要麦克风权限',
            content: '跟读评分需要使用麦克风，请在设置中开启。',
            confirmText: '去设置',
            success: (r) => {
              if (r.confirm) wx.openSetting({})
            }
          })
          return
        }
        if (auth['scope.record'] === true) {
          cb()
          return
        }
        wx.authorize({
          scope: 'scope.record',
          success: cb,
          fail: () => wx.showToast({ title: '未获得麦克风权限', icon: 'none' })
        })
      },
      fail: cb
    })
  },

  beginRecord(index) {
    this.recIndex = index
    this.recStart = Date.now()
    this.setData({ recording: true, recIndex: index, recSeconds: 0 })
    this.recTimer = setInterval(() => {
      this.setData({ recSeconds: Math.floor((Date.now() - this.recStart) / 1000) })
    }, 500)
    try {
      this.recorder.start({
        duration: 60000,
        sampleRate: 16000,
        numberOfChannels: 1,
        encodeBitRate: 48000,
        format: 'mp3'
      })
    } catch (err) {
      this.setData({ recording: false, recIndex: -1 })
      wx.showToast({ title: '录音启动失败', icon: 'none' })
    }
  },

  stopRecord() {
    if (!this.recorder) return
    try {
      this.recorder.stop()
    } catch (e) {
      this.setData({ recording: false, recIndex: -1 })
    }
  },

  stopRecordSilently() {
    if (this.data.recording && this.recorder) {
      try {
        this.recorder.stop()
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (this.recTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.recTimer)
      this.recTimer = null
    }
  },

  handleRecordStop() {
    if (this.recTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.recTimer)
      this.recTimer = null
    }
    if (this.recIndex < 0) {
      this.setData({ recording: false, recIndex: -1 })
      return
    }
    const index = this.recIndex
    const seconds = Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - this.recStart) / 100) / 10)
    const sentence = this.data.material.sentences[index]
    const scored = util.scoreShadowing(sentence.en, seconds)
    const scores = Object.assign({}, this.data.sentenceScores)
    scores[index] = {
      score: scored.score,
      stars: scored.stars,
      wpm: scored.wpm,
      duration: scored.duration
    }
    const keys = Object.keys(scores)
    let total = 0
    keys.forEach((k) => {
      total += scores[k].score
    })
    this.recIndex = -1
    this.setData({
      recording: false,
      recIndex: -1,
      recSeconds: 0,
      sentenceScores: scores,
      shadowCount: keys.length,
      shadowAvg: keys.length ? Math.round(total / keys.length) : 0
    })
    if (this._active) {
      wx.showToast({
        title: '跟读评分 ' + scored.score + ' 分 · ' + scored.wpm + ' 词/分',
        icon: 'none',
        duration: 1600
      })
    }
  },

  /* ---------------- 听力题 ---------------- */
  startQuiz() {
    if (this.data.shadowCount === 0) {
      wx.showModal({
        title: '还没有跟读',
        content: '跟读一句可以获得更完整的训练反馈，也可以直接进入听力题。',
        confirmText: '直接做题',
        cancelText: '先跟读',
        success: (res) => {
          if (res.confirm) this.beginQuiz()
        }
      })
      return
    }
    this.beginQuiz()
  },

  beginQuiz() {
    const q = this.data.material.questions[0]
    this.setData({
      phase: 'quiz',
      quizIndex: 0,
      quizCorrect: 0,
      quizTotal: this.data.material.questions.length,
      quizQuestion: q,
      quizOptions: q.options.map((text, i) => ({ text: text, letter: 'ABCD'[i] })),
      quizSelected: -1,
      quizAnswered: false
    })
    this.quizStart = Date.now()
  },

  chooseQuiz(e) {
    if (this.data.quizAnswered) return
    const idx = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    const correct = idx === this.data.quizQuestion.answer
    this.setData({
      quizSelected: idx,
      quizAnswered: true,
      quizCorrect: this.data.quizCorrect + (correct ? 1 : 0)
    })
    if (correct) store.vibrate('light')
  },

  nextQuiz() {
    const next = this.data.quizIndex + 1
    if (next >= this.data.quizTotal) {
      this.finish()
      return
    }
    this.setData({
      quizIndex: next,
      quizQuestion: this.data.material.questions[next],
      quizOptions: this.data.material.questions[next].options.map((text, i) => ({ text
: text, letter: 'ABCD'[i] })),
      quizSelected: -1,
      quizAnswered: false
    })
  },

  finish() {
    this.finished = true
    const total = this.data.quizTotal
    const correct = this.data.quizCorrect
    const accuracy = total ? Math.round((correct / total) * 100) : 0
    const exp = 15 + this.data.shadowCount * 5 + correct * 8
    store.saveListeningScore(this.data.material.id, this.data.shadowAvg)
    store.logActivity({
      exp: exp,
      fish: 8,
      correct: correct,
      wrong: total - correct,
      minutes: Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - (this.startedAt || Date.now())) / 
60000)),
      quizzes: total
    })
    this.setData({
      phase: 'done',
      result: {
        shadowAvg: this.data.shadowAvg,
        shadowCount: this.data.shadowCount,
        correct: correct,
        total: total,
        accuracy: accuracy,
        exp: exp,
        stars: this.data.shadowAvg >= 90 ? 5 : this.data.shadowAvg >= 80 ? 4 : this.da
ta.shadowAvg >= 70 ? 3 : 2
      }
    })
    this.stopTimers()
  },

  restart() {
    this.finished = false
    this.selectMaterialById(this.data.activeId)
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
  },

  goHome() {
    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
  },

  stopTimers() {
    if (this.playTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.playTimer)
      this.playTimer = null
    }
    if (this.playSentenceTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.playSentenceTimer)
      this.playSentenceTimer = null
    }
    if (this.recTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.recTimer)
      this.recTimer = null
    }
  }
})


/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/listening/listening.json
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
{
  "navigationBarTitleText": "影子跟读"
}


/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/listening/listening.wxml
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
<view class="page listening-page">
  <!-- 材料切换 -->
  <scroll-view scroll-x class="mat-scroll" enhanced show-scrollbar="{{false}}">
    <view class="mat-row">
      <view
        wx:for="{{materials}}"
        wx:key="id"
        class="mat-chip {{activeId === item.id ? 'is-active' : ''}}"
        data-id="{{item.id}}"
        bindtap="selectMaterial"
      >
        <text class="mat-emoji">{{item.emoji}}</text>
        <text class="mat-name">{{item.title}}</text>
      </view>
    </view>
  </scroll-view>

  <!-- 跟读阶段 -->
  <block wx:if="{{phase === 'listen'}}">
    <view class="player-card">
      <view class="player-hd">
        <text class="player-emoji">{{material.emoji}}</text>
        <view class="flex1">
          <view class="player-title">{{material.title}}</view>
          <view class="player-sub">{{material.cnTitle}} · {{material.level}}</view>
        </view>
        <view class="level-chip">{{material.questions.length}} 题</view>
      </view>

        <view class="wave-item"></view>

        <view class="wave-item"></view>
        <view class="wave-item"></view>
        <view class="wave-item"></view>
        <view class="wave-item"></view>
      </view>

      <view class="bar bar-thin">
        <view class="bar-inner" style="width:{{playProgress}}%"></view>
      </view>
      <view class="player-time">
        <text>{{playTimeText}}</text>
        <view class="play-btn" bindtap="togglePlay">{{playing ? '⏸ 暂停' : '▶ 播放'}}<
/view>
        <text>{{totalTimeText}}</text>
      </view>
      <view class="player-tip">先盲听一遍，再逐句跟读，效果更好</view>
    </view>

    <view class="script-hd">

      <text class="script-toggle" bindtap="toggleScript">{{showScript ? '隐藏译文' : '
显示译文'}}</text>
    </view>

    <view class="sentences">
      <view
        wx:for="{{material.sentences}}"
        wx:key="en"
        class="sentence {{playingIndex === index ? 'is-playing' : ''}}"
      >
        <view class="sent-index">{{index + 1}}</view>
        <view class="flex1">
          <text class="sent-en">{{item.en}}</text>
          <text wx:if="{{showScript}}" class="sent-cn">{{item.cn}}</text>
          <view wx:if="{{sentenceScores[index]}}" class="sent-score">
            ⭐ {{sentenceScores[index].stars}} · {{sentenceScores[index].score}} 分 · {
{sentenceScores[index].wpm}} 词/分
          </view>
        </view>
        <view class="sent-actions">
          <view class="sent-btn" data-index="{{index}}" bindtap="playSentence">▶</view
>
          <view
            class="sent-btn mic-btn {{recording && recIndex === index ? 'is-rec' : ''}
}"
            data-index="{{index}}"
            bindtap="startShadow"
          >{{recording && recIndex === index ? '■' : '🎤'}}</view>
        </view>
      </view>
    </view>

    <view wx:if="{{recording}}" class="rec-bar">
      <view class="rec-dot"></view>
      <text class="rec-text">正在跟读第 {{recIndex + 1}} 句 · {{recSeconds}}s</text>
      <view class="rec-stop" bindtap="stopRecord">停止并评分</view>
    </view>

    <view class="shadow-summary">
      <view class="shadow-cell">
        <text class="num">{{shadowAvg}}</text>
        <text class="shadow-label">跟读均分</text>
      </view>

      </view>

  </block>

  <!-- 听力题阶段 -->
  <block wx:elif="{{phase === 'quiz'}}">
    <view class="quiz-top">
      <text class="quiz-progress">第 {{quizIndex + 1}} / {{quizTotal}} 题</text>
      <text class="quiz-correct">✓ {{quizCorrect}}</text>
    </view>
    <view class="quiz-card">
      <view class="quiz-q">{{quizQuestion.q}}</view>
      <view class="options">
        <view
          wx:for="{{quizOptions}}"
          wx:key="letter"
          class="option {{quizAnswered && index === quizQuestion.answer ? 'is-correct'
 : ''}} {{quizAnswered && index === quizSelected && index !== quizQuestion.answer ? 'i
s-wrong' : ''}}"
          data-index="{{index}}"
          bindtap="chooseQuiz"
        >
          <view class="option-letter">{{item.letter}}</view>
          <text class="option-text">{{item.text}}</text>
        </view>
      </view>
      <view wx:if="{{quizAnswered}}" class="explain">
        <text class="explain-title">{{quizSelected === quizQuestion.answer ? '🎉 正确'
 : '📝 解析'}}</text>
        <text class="explain-text">{{quizQuestion.explain}}</text>
      </view>
    </view>
    <view wx:if="{{quizAnswered}}" class="btn btn-primary btn-block" bindtap="nextQuiz
">
      {{quizIndex + 1 >= quizTotal ? '查看本次成绩' : '下一题'}}
    </view>
  </block>

  <!-- 结算 -->
  <block wx:else>
    <view class="done-card">
      <view class="sticker sticker-blue">听力训练完成</view>
      <view class="done-stars">
        <text wx:for="{{[1,2,3,4,5]}}" wx:key="*this" class="star {{index < result.sta
rs ? 'is-on' : ''}}">★</text>
      </view>
      <view class="done-main">{{result.shadowAvg}}<text class="done-unit">分</text></v
iew>
      <view class="done-label">跟读平均分</view>
      <view class="result-grid">
        <view class="result-cell">
          <text class="num">{{result.shadowCount}}</text>
          <text class="result-label">跟读句数</text>
        </view>
        <view class="result-cell">
          <text class="num">{{result.correct}}/{{result.total}}</text>
          <text class="result-label">听力答对</text>
        </view>
        <view class="result-cell">
          <text class="num">{{result.accuracy}}%</text>
          <text class="result-label">正确率</text>
        </view>
        <view class="result-cell">
          <text class="num">+{{result.exp}}</text>
          <text class="result-label">词力</text>
        </view>
      </view>
      <view class="done-tip">跟读评分结合了语速与句子长度，仅作为练习参考。坚持影子跟
读，语音语调会越来越自然。</view>
      <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block" bindtap="restart">再练一次</view>
      <view class="btn btn-ghost btn-block mt-16" bindtap="goHome">回到小岛</view>
    </view>
  </block>
</view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/pet/pet.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 伙伴小窝
 * 5 只原创伙伴、每只 5 段进化 + 2 条最终分支；喂食、抚摸、换装、切换伙伴。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')

const ACCESSORIES = [
  { id: 'none', name: '不戴配饰', emoji: '🚫', level: 1 },
  { id: 'hat', name: '学士帽', emoji: '🎩', level: 3 },
  { id: 'scarf', name: '暖暖围巾', emoji: '🧣', level: 5 },
  { id: 'crown', name: '伙伴王冠', emoji: '👑', level: 8 },
  { id: 'wings', name: '星光翅膀', emoji: '✨', level: 12 }
]

Page({
  data: {
    pet: null,
    collection: [],
    nextPet: null,
    ownedCount: 0,
    fish: 0,
    expPercent: 0,
    stages: [],
    branches: [],
    profile: null,
    accessories: [],
    showWardrobe: false,
    hearts: false,
    todayLearned: 0,
    totalLearned: 0
  },

  onShow() {
    if (typeof this.getTabBar === 'function' && this.getTabBar()) {
      this.getTabBar().setSelected(2)
    }
    this.refresh()
  },

  refresh() {
    const state = store.getState()
    const pet = store.getPetView()
    const collection = store.getPetCollection()
    const stages = store.getStages()
    const branches = store.getPetBranches()
    const profile = store.getVocabProfile()
    const accessories = ACCESSORIES.map((a) => {
      const unlocked = pet.unlocked.indexOf(a.id) >= 0
      const active = pet.accessory === a.id
      return {
        id: a.id,
        name: a.name,
        emoji: a.emoji,
        level: a.level,
        unlocked: unlocked,
        active: active,
        statusText: unlocked ? (active ? '正在佩戴' : '点击佩戴') : 'Lv.' + a.level + 
' 解锁'
      }
    })
    this.setData({
      pet: pet,
      collection: collection,
      nextPet: collection.filter((p) => !p.owned)[0] || null,
      ownedCount: collection.filter((p) => p.owned).length,
      fish: state.wallet.fish,
      expPercent: Math.round((pet.expInLevel / pet.expNeed) * 100),
      stages: stages,
      branches: branches,
      profile: profile,
      accessories: accessories,
      todayLearned: store.getToday().newWords,
      totalLearned: state.stats.totalLearned
    })
  },

  switchPet(e) {
    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id
    const res = store.switchPet(id)
    if (!res.ok) {
      wx.showToast({ title: res.msg, icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    this.setData({ hearts: true })
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.setData({ hearts: false })
    }, 900)
    wx.showToast({ title: res.msg, icon: 'none' })
    this.refresh()
  },

  onPetTap() {
    const res = store.petPet()
    this.setData({ hearts: true })
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.setData({ hearts: false })
    }, 1200)
    wx.showToast({ title: res.msg, icon: 'none' })
    this.refresh()
  },

  feed() {
    const res = store.feedPet()
    wx.showToast({ title: res.msg, icon: 'none' })
    if (res.ok) {
      store.vibrate('light')
      this.refresh()
    }
  },

  openWardrobe() {
    this.setData({ showWardrobe: true })
  },

  closeWardrobe() {
    this.setData({ showWardrobe: false })
  },

  chooseAccessory(e) {
    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id
    const item = this.data.accessories.filter((a) => a.id === id)[0]
    if (!item) return
    if (!item.unlocked) {
      wx.showToast({ title: this.data.pet.name + ' Lv.' + item.level + ' 解锁', icon: 
'none' })
      return
    }
    store.setAccessory(id)
    this.refresh()
    wx.showToast({ title: '换好啦', icon: 'none' })
  },

  goGarden() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/garden/garden' })
  },

  goStudy() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/study/study' })
  },

  noop() {}
})

 * 文件：pages/pet/pet.json
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */

  "navigationBarTitleText": "伙伴小窝"

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/pet/pet.wxml
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
<view class="page page-tab pet-page">
  <!-- 伙伴切换 -->
  <scroll-view scroll-x class="pet-scroll" enhanced show-scrollbar="{{false}}">
    <view class="pet-switch">
      <view
        wx:for="{{collection}}"
        wx:key="id"
        class="pet-tab {{item.active ? 'is-active' : ''}} {{item.owned ? '' : 'is-lock
ed'}}"
        data-id="{{item.id}}"
        bindtap="switchPet"
      >
        <text class="pet-tab-emoji">{{item.emoji}}</text>
        <view class="pet-tab-info">
          <text class="pet-tab-name">{{item.name}}</text>
          <text class="pet-tab-sub">{{item.owned ? item.stageName : '未解锁'}}</text>
        </view>
        <view wx:if="{{item.active}}" class="pet-tab-dot"></view>
      </view>
    </view>
  </scroll-view>

  <!-- 伙伴生活场景 -->
  <view class="sky">
    <view class="sun"></view>
    <view class="cloud cloud-1"></view>
    <view class="cloud cloud-2"></view>
    <view class="hill hill-back"></view>
    <view class="hill hill-front"></view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/plan/plan.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 备考计划
 * 根据考试日期、每日新词量与目标分数，生成分阶段日程与每日配速。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const planUtil = require('../../utils/plan.js')
const wordsData = require('../../data/words.js')

Page({
  data: {
    examDate: '',
    dailyNew: 15,
    targetScore: 500,
    targetScores: [425, 500, 550, 600],
    targetIndex: 1,
    daysLeft: 60,
    progress: { total: 0, learned: 0, mastered: 0 },
    plan: null,
    phases: [],
    schedule: [],
    today: '',
    phaseName: '',
    changed: false
  },

  onLoad() {
    const state = store.getState()
    const profile = state.profile
    const index = Math.max(0, this.data.targetScores.indexOf(profile.targetScore))
    this.setData({
      examDate: profile.examDate,
      dailyNew: profile.dailyNew,
      targetScore: profile.targetScore,
      targetIndex: index,
      today: util.formatDate(new Date(), 'YYYY-MM-DD')
    })
    this.build()
  },

  build() {
    const state = store.getState()
    const profile = {
      examDate: this.data.examDate,
      dailyNew: this.data.dailyNew,
      targetScore: this.data.targetScore
    }
    const progress = store.getBookProgress(wordsData.all)
    const plan = planUtil.buildPlan(profile, progress)
    const phases = planUtil.getPhases(profile).map((p) => {
      const left = plan.totalDays
      let current = false
      if (p.key === 'sprint' && left <= 14) current = true
      if (p.key === 'boost' && left > 14 && left <= 30) current = true
      if (p.key === 'base' && left > 30) current = true
      return Object.assign({}, p, { current: current, days: Math.max(0, p.days) })
    })
    const schedule = plan.schedule.map((item) => {
      let stageClass = 'green'
      if (item.stage === '真题强化') stageClass = 'orange'
      if (item.stage === '冲刺模考') stageClass = 'pink'
      if (item.stage === '周复盘') stageClass = 'blue'
      return Object.assign({}, item, { stageClass: stageClass })
    })
    this.setData({
      progress: progress,
      plan: plan,
      daysLeft: plan.totalDays,
      phaseName: plan.totalDays <= 14 ? '冲刺模考' : plan.totalDays <= 30 ? '真题强化'
 : '基础积累',
      phases: phases,
      schedule: schedule
    })
  },

  onDateChange(e) {
    this.setData({ examDate: e.detail.value, changed: true })
    this.build()
  },

  onTargetChange(e) {
    const index = Number(e.detail.value)
    this.setData({
      targetIndex: index,
      targetScore: this.data.targetScores[index],
      changed: true
    })
  },

  decrease() {
    const n = Math.max(5, this.data.dailyNew - 5)
    this.setData({ dailyNew: n, changed: true })
    this.build()
  },

  increase() {
    const n = Math.min(60, this.data.dailyNew + 5)
    this.setData({ dailyNew: n, changed: true })
    this.build()
  },

  save() {
    const state = store.getState()
    state.profile.examDate = this.data.examDate
    state.profile.dailyNew = this.data.dailyNew
    state.profile.targetScore = this.data.targetScore
    store.save()
    this.setData({ changed: false })
    wx.showToast({ title: '计划已保存', icon: 'success' })
  },

  goStudy() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/study/study' })
  },

  goExam() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/exam/exam' })
  }
})
