/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/profile/profile.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 我的 · 个人中心
 * 资料编辑、学习概览与各功能入口。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const quotes = require('../../data/quotes.js')

const AVATARS = ['🐣', '🦊', '🐼', '🐸', '🐙', '🦄', '🐯', '🐨']
const MENU = [
  { icon: '🎯', name: '备考计划', desc: '倒计时与每日配速', url: '/pages/plan/plan', c
olor: 'orange' },
  { icon: '📊', name: '学习统计', desc: '数据、热力图与模块完成度', url: '/pages/stats
/stats', color: 'blue' },
  { icon: '🏅', name: '成就墙', desc: '收集属于你的徽章', url: '/pages/achievements/ac
hievements', color: 'yellow' },
  { icon: '🩺', name: '错题病历本', desc: '按错因生成复习处方', url: '/pages/wrongbook
/wrongbook', color: 'pink' },
  { icon: '🌷', name: '知识花园', desc: '看看单词开出的花', url: '/pages/garden/garden
', color: 'green' },
  { icon: '📜', name: '剧情闯关', desc: '词灵岛主线故事', url: '/pages/chapters/chapte
rs', color: 'purple' }
]

Page({
  data: {
    profile: null,
    level: 1,
    stats: null,
    achCount: 0,
    achTotal: 0,
    gardenCount: 0,
    wrongCount: 0,
    fish: 0,
    joinDays: 1,
    quote: null,
    menu: MENU,
    showEdit: false,
    tempName: '',
    tempAvatar: '🐣',
    avatars: AVATARS,
    version: '1.0.0'
  },

  onShow() {
    if (typeof this.getTabBar === 'function' && this.getTabBar()) {
      this.getTabBar().setSelected(3)
    }
    this.refresh()
  },

  refresh() {
    const state = store.getState()
    const profile = state.profile
    const achievements = store.getAchievements()
    const joinDays = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((Date.now() - profile.joinedAt) / 86400000)
)
    this.setData({
      profile: profile,
      level: store.getPetLevel(),
      stats: store.getStatsView(),
      achCount: achievements.filter((a) => a.unlocked).length,
      achTotal: achievements.length,
      gardenCount: store.getGardenView().count,
      wrongCount: store.getWrongStats().total,
      fish: state.wallet.fish,
      joinDays: joinDays,
      quote: util.dailyQuote(quotes)
    })
  },

  openEdit() {
    this.setData({
      showEdit: true,
      tempName: this.data.profile.nickname,
      tempAvatar: this.data.profile.avatar
    })
  },

  closeEdit() {
    this.setData({ showEdit: false })
  },

  chooseAvatar(e) {
    this.setData({ tempAvatar: e.currentTarget.dataset.avatar })
  },

  onNameInput(e) {
    this.setData({ tempName: e.detail.value })
  },

  saveProfile() {
    const name = (this.data.tempName || '').trim()
    if (!name) {
      wx.showToast({ title: '昵称不能为空', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    const state = store.getState()
    state.profile.nickname = name.slice(0, 12)
    state.profile.avatar = this.data.tempAvatar
    store.save()
    this.setData({ showEdit: false })
    this.refresh()
    wx.showToast({ title: '资料已更新', icon: 'none' })
  },

  goUrl(e) {
    const url = e.currentTarget.dataset.url
    if (url) wx.navigateTo({ url: url })
  },

  goSettings() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/settings/settings' })
  },

  goAbout() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/about/about' })
  },

  goStudy() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/study/study' })
  },

  onShareAppMessage() {
    return {
      title: '词灵岛 · 四六级卡通英语学习，边养伙伴边背单词',
      path: '/pages/index/index'
    }
  },

  noop() {}
})


/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/profile/profile.json

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/puzzle/puzzle.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 词根拼图工坊
 * 将单词拆成词根/词缀碎片，混入干扰项，让学习者按顺序拼回单词。
 * 这是本产品区别于普通背词软件的核心玩法之一。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const wordsData = require('../../data/words.js')

const DISTRACTORS = [
  'un-', 're-', 'pre-', 'dis-', 'sub-', 'inter-', 'en-',
  '-tion', '-ment', '-able', '-ous', '-ive', '-ly', '-al', '-ful', '-less'
]

function normalize(str) {
  return (str || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, '')
}

Page({
  data: {
    levels: [],
    total: 0,
    index: 0,
    level: null,
    slots: [],
    pool: [],
    built: [],
    hintUid: '',
    score: 0,
    combo: 0,
    best: 0,
    progress: 0,
    phase: 'play',
    status: 'idle',
    shake: false,
    showResult: false,
    gained: 0,
    lastLevel: null
  },

  onLoad() {
    this.buildLevels()
  },

  /** 仅挑选词根可完整还原单词的条目，保证拼图有唯一解 */
  buildLevels() {
    const list = wordsData.all.filter((w) => {
      if (!w.roots || w.roots.length < 2) return false
      const joined = normalize(w.roots.map((r) => r.t).join(''))
      return joined === normalize(w.word)
    })
    const picked = []
    const step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(list.length / 12))
    for (let i = 0; i < list.length && picked.length < 12; i += step) {
      picked.push(list[i])
    }
    this.setData({
      levels: picked,
      total: picked.length,
      best: store.getState().puzzle.best,
      score: 0,
      combo: 0,
      phase: 'play'
    })
    if (picked.length) this.startLevel(0)
  },

  startLevel(i) {
    const levels = this.data.levels
    const level = levels[i]
    if (!level) return
    const correct = level.roots.map((r, idx) => ({
      uid: 'c' + idx,
      t: r.t,
      correct: true,
      used: false
    }))
    const poolExtra = DISTRACTORS.filter((d) => {
      return !level.roots.some((r) => r.t === d)
    })
    const wrong = util.sample(poolExtra, 2).map((t, idx) => ({
      uid: 'w' + idx + '_' + i,
      t: t,
      correct: false,
      used: false
    }))
    const pool = util.shuffle(correct.concat(wrong))
    const slots = []
    for (let k = 0; k < correct.length; k++) {
      slots.push({ key: 'slot_' + k, piece: null })
    }
    this.setData({
      index: i,
      level: level,
      pool: pool,
      slots: slots,
      built: [],
      status: 'idle',
      shake: false,
      showResult: false,
      hintUid: '',
      usedHint: false,
      progress: Math.round(((i + 1) / levels.length) * 100)
    })
  },

  tapPiece(e) {
    if (this.data.showResult) return
    const uid = e.currentTarget.dataset.uid
    const piece = this.data.pool.filter((p) => p.uid === uid && !p.used)[0]
    if (!piece) return
    if (this.data.built.length >= this.data.slots.length) {
      wx.showToast({ title: '已经拼满啦，检查一下？', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    const pool = this.data.pool.map((p) => (p.uid === uid ? Object.assign({}, p, { use
d: true }) : p))
    const built = this.data.built.concat([piece])
    const slots = this.data.slots.slice()
    slots[built.length - 1] = { key: slots[built.length - 1].key, piece: piece }
    this.setData({
      pool: pool,
      built: built,
      slots: slots,
      hintUid: '',
      status: 'idle'
    })
  },

  undo() {
    if (!this.data.built.length) return
    const built = this.data.built.slice()
    const last = built.pop()
    const pool = this.data.pool.map((p) => (p.uid === last.uid ? Object.assign({}, p, 
{ used: false }) : p))
    const slots = this.data.slots.slice()
    slots[built.length] = { key: slots[built.length].key, piece: null }
    this.setData({ built: built, pool: pool, slots: slots, status: 'idle' })
  },

  clearAll() {
    const pool = this.data.pool.map((p) => Object.assign({}, p, { used: false }))
    const slots = this.data.slots.map((s) => ({ key: s.key, piece: null }))
    this.setData({ built: [], pool: pool, slots: slots, status: 'idle' })
  },

  hint() {
    if (!this.data.level) return
    const nextIndex = this.data.built.length
    const root = this.data.level.roots[nextIndex]
    if (!root) return
    const target = this.data.pool.filter((p) => !p.used && p.t === root.t)[0]
    if (!target) return
    this.setData({ hintUid: target.uid, usedHint: true })
    wx.showToast({ title: '发光的这块就是下一块', icon: 'none' })
  },

  check() {
    if (this.data.built.length < this.data.slots.length) {
      wx.showToast({ title: '还有空位没拼满哦', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    const answer = normalize(this.data.built.map((b) => b.t).join(''))
    if (answer === normalize(this.data.level.word)) {
      const gained = Math.max(4, 10 + this.data.combo * 2 - (this.data.usedHint ? 3 : 
0))
      const score = this.data.score + gained
      store.savePuzzleResult(score, this.data.level.id)
      store.logActivity({ exp: 12, fish: 4, correct: 1, minutes: 1 })
      store.vibrate('light')
      this.setData({
        status: 'right',
        showResult: true,
        score: score,
        combo: this.data.combo + 1,
        gained: gained,
        usedHint: false,
        lastLevel: this.data.level
      })
      return
    }
    this.setData({ status: 'wrong', combo: 0, shake: true })
    store.vibrate('heavy')
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.setData({ shake: false })
    }, 500)
    wx.showToast({ title: '顺序不对，再想想词根含义', icon: 'none' })
  },

  next() {
    if (this.data.index + 1 >= this.data.total) {
      this.finish()
      return
    }
    this.startLevel(this.data.index + 1)
  },

  finish() {
    const best = Math.max(this.data.best, this.data.score)
    this.setData({
      phase: 'done',
      best: best,
      showResult: false
    })
  },

  restart() {
    this.buildLevels()
  },

  goHome() {
    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
  },

  noop() {}
})


/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/puzzle/puzzle.json
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
{
  "navigationBarTitleText": "词根拼图"
}


/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/puzzle/puzzle.wxml
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
<view class="page puzzle-page">
  <!-- 顶部状态 -->
  <view class="puzzle-top">
    <view class="progress-wrap">
      <view class="bar bar-thin bar-purple">
        <view class="bar-inner" style="width:{{progress}}%"></view>
      </view>
      <text class="progress-text">{{index + 1}} / {{total}}</text>
    </view>
    <view class="score-chip">⭐ {{score}}</view>
    <view wx:if="{{combo > 1}}" class="combo-chip">连击 x{{combo}}</view>
  </view>

  <block wx:if="{{phase === 'play'}}">
    <!-- 目标词 -->
    <view class="target-card">
      <view class="target-tag">拼出这个单词</view>
      <view class="target-cn">{{level.word.cn}}</view>
      <view class="target-meta">{{level.word.pos}} · {{level.word.phonetic}}</view>
      <view class="slots {{shake ? 'is-shake' : ''}}">
        <view
          wx:for="{{slots}}"
          wx:key="key"
          class="slot {{item.piece ? 'is-filled' : ''}}"
        >
          <text wx:if="{{item.piece}}" class="slot-text">{{item.piece.t}}</text>
          <text wx:else class="slot-empty">?</text>
        </view>
        <view class="slot-plus">+</view>
      </view>
      <view class="target-hint">词根之间可能有干扰项，按正确顺序点击</view>
    </view>

    <!-- 碎片池 -->
    <view class="pool-title">
      <text class="pool-label">词根碎片</text>
      <text class="pool-sub">已选 {{built.length}}/{{slots.length}}</text>
    </view>
    <view class="pool">
      <view
        wx:for="{{pool}}"
        wx:key="uid"
        class="piece {{item.used ? 'is-used' : ''}} {{hintUid === item.uid ? 'is-hint'
 : ''}}"
        data-uid="{{item.uid}}"
        bindtap="tapPiece"
      >
        <text>{{item.t}}</text>
      </view>
    </view>

    <!-- 操作 -->
    <view class="controls">
      <view class="ctrl" bindtap="undo">↩ 撤销</view>
      <view class="ctrl" bindtap="clearAll">✕ 清空</view>
      <view class="ctrl" bindtap="hint">💡 提示</view>
    </view>

    <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block check-btn" bindtap="check">检查拼写</view>
  </block>

  <!-- 结算 -->
  <block wx:else>
    <view class="done-card">
      <view class="sticker sticker-purple">本轮完成</view>
      <view class="done-emoji">🧩</view>
      <view class="done-score">{{score}}</view>
      <view class="done-label">本轮得分</view>
      <view class="done-best">历史最佳 {{best}} 分</view>
      <view class="done-tip">词根拆解能帮你把「记一个词」变成「记一串词」。明天再来挑
战更高连击吧！</view>
      <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block" bindtap="restart">再来一轮</view>
      <view class="btn btn-ghost btn-block mt-16" bindtap="goHome">回到小岛</view>
    </view>
  </block>

  <!-- 拼写成功 -->
  <view wx:if="{{showResult}}" class="mask" catchtap="noop">
    <view class="modal result-modal">
      <view class="result-spark">✦</view>
      <view class="result-title">拼对啦！ +{{gained}} 分</view>
      <view class="result-word">{{lastLevel.word.word}}</view>
      <view class="result-phonetic">{{lastLevel.word.phonetic}} · {{lastLevel.word.cn}
}</view>
      <view class="result-example">{{lastLevel.word.en}}</view>
      <view class="result-root">
        <view wx:for="{{lastLevel.roots}}" wx:key="t" class="result-root-item">
          <text class="result-root-t">{{item.t}}</text>
          <text class="result-root-m">{{item.m}}</text>
        </view>
      </view>
      <view class="result-tip">💡 {{lastLevel.word.tip}}</view>
      <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block mt-24" bindtap="next">
        {{index + 1 >= total ? '查看本轮成绩' : '下一关'}}
      </view>
    </view>
  </view>
</view>


/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/puzzle/puzzle.wxss
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/* 词根拼图 */
.puzzle-page {
  min-height: 100vh;
}

.puzzle-top {
  display: flex;

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/reading/reading.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 阅读闯关
 * 限时阅读 + 生词点查 + 逐题解析，完成后记录用时与正确率。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const audio = require('../../utils/audio.js')
const readings = require('../../data/readings.js')

Page({
  data: {
    readings: readings.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, title: r.title, emoji: r.emoji })),
    activeId: 'r1',
    article: null,
    phase: 'read',
    elapsedText: '0 分钟',
    quizIndex: 0,
    quizQuestion: null,
    quizOptions: [],
    quizSelected: -1,
    quizAnswered: false,
    quizCorrect: 0,
    result: null,
    showWord: false,
    wordInfo: null
  },

  onLoad(options) {
    const id = options && options.id ? options.id : 'r1'
    this.loadArticle(id)
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
  },

  onUnload() {
    this.stopTimer()
    if (this.data.phase !== 'done' && this.startedAt) {
      const minutes = (Date.now() - this.startedAt) / 60000
      if (minutes >= 1) store.addMinutes(minutes)
    }
  },

  loadArticle(id) {
    const article = readings.filter((r) => r.id === id)[0] || readings[0]
    this.setData({
      activeId: article.id,
      article: article,
      phase: 'read',
      quizIndex: 0,
      quizCorrect: 0,
      quizSelected: -1,
      quizAnswered: false,
      result: null,
      showWord: false
    })
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
    this.startTimer()
  },

  startTimer() {
    this.stopTimer()
    this.timer = setInterval(() => {
      const minutes = Math.floor((Date.now() - this.startedAt) / 60000)
      this.setData({ elapsedText: minutes + ' 分钟' })
    }, 5000)
  },

  stopTimer() {
    if (this.timer) {
      clearInterval(this.timer)
      this.timer = null
    }
  },

  selectArticle(e) {
    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id
    if (id === this.data.activeId) return
    this.loadArticle(id)
  },

  showWord(e) {
    const index = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    const word = this.data.article.keyWords[index]
    if (!word) return
    this.setData({ showWord: true, wordInfo: word })
  },

  closeWord() {
    this.setData({ showWord: false })
  },

  speakWord() {
    if (!this.data.wordInfo) return
    audio.playWord(this.data.wordInfo.word)
    wx.showToast({ title: this.data.wordInfo.word, icon: 'none' })
  },

  startQuiz() {
    this.stopTimer()
    const q = this.data.article.questions[0]
    this.setData({
      phase: 'quiz',
      quizIndex: 0,
      quizQuestion: q,
      quizOptions: q.options.map((text, i) => ({ text: text, letter: 'ABCD'[i] })),
      quizSelected: -1,
      quizAnswered: false
    })
  },

  choose(e) {
    if (this.data.quizAnswered) return
    const idx = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    const correct = idx === this.data.quizQuestion.answer
    this.setData({
      quizSelected: idx,
      quizAnswered: true,
      quizCorrect: this.data.quizCorrect + (correct ? 1 : 0)
    })
    if (correct) store.vibrate('light')
  },

  next() {
    const next = this.data.quizIndex + 1
    if (next >= this.data.article.questions.length) {
      this.finish()
      return
    }
    const q = this.data.article.questions[next]
    this.setData({
      quizIndex: next,
      quizQuestion: q,
      quizOptions: q.options.map((text, i) => ({ text: text, letter: 'ABCD'[i] })),
      quizSelected: -1,
      quizAnswered: false
    })
  },

  finish() {
    const total = this.data.article.questions.length
    const correct = this.data.quizCorrect
    const accuracy = total ? Math.round((correct / total) * 100) : 0
    const minutes = Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - this.startedAt) / 60000))
    const exp = 12 + correct * 10
    store.logActivity({
      correct: correct,
      wrong: total - correct,
      minutes: minutes,
      quizzes: total,
      exp: exp,
      fish: 8
    })
    store.markFinished('readings', this.data.article.id)
    this.setData({
      phase: 'done',
      result: {
        total: total,
        correct: correct,
        accuracy: accuracy,
        minutes: minutes,
        exp: exp,
        comment:
          accuracy === 100
            ? '全对！这篇文章的生词已经被你拿下了。'
            : accuracy >= 60
            ? '理解得不错，回看错题所在的段落会更牢。'
            : '别灰心，先把关键生词过一遍，再读一次文章。'
      }
    })
    this.stopTimer()
  },

  retry() {
    this.loadArticle(this.data.activeId)
  },

  goHome() {
    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
  },

  noop() {}
})

<view class="page reading-page">
  <!-- 文章选择 -->
  <scroll-view scroll-x class="article-scroll" enhanced show-scrollbar="{{false}}">
    <view class="article-row">
      <view
        wx:for="{{readings}}"
        wx:key="id"
        class="article-chip {{activeId === item.id ? 'is-active' : ''}}"
        data-id="{{item.id}}"
        bindtap="selectArticle"
      >
        <text class="article-chip-emoji">{{item.emoji}}</text>
        <text class="article-chip-title">{{item.title}}</text>
      </view>
    </view>
  </scroll-view>

  <!-- 阅读阶段 -->
  <block wx:if="{{phase === 'read'}}">
    <view class="paper">
      <view class="paper-hd">
        <text class="paper-emoji">{{article.emoji}}</text>
        <view class="flex1">
          <view class="paper-title">{{article.title}}</view>
          <view class="paper-sub">{{article.cnTitle}} · {{article.level}}</view>
        </view>
        <view class="timer-chip">⏱ {{elapsedText}}</view>
      </view>

      <view class="paper-meta">
        <text class="tag tag-blue">约 {{article.minutes}} 分钟</text>
        <text class="tag tag-green">{{article.questions.length}} 道理解题</text>
        <text class="tag tag-orange">{{article.keyWords.length}} 个生词</text>
      </view>

      <view class="passage">
        <text wx:for="{{article.passage}}" wx:key="*this" class="paragraph">{{item}}</
text>
      </view>

      <view class="keyword-box">
        <view class="keyword-title">🔍 点一点生词</view>
        <view class="keyword-row">
          <view
            wx:for="{{article.keyWords}}"
            wx:key="word"
            class="keyword"
            data-index="{{index}}"
            bindtap="showWord"
          >{{item.word}}</view>
        </view>
      </view>
    </view>

    <view class="read-footer">
      <text class="read-hint">读完再答题，正确率会更高</text>
      <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block" bindtap="startQuiz">开始答题</view>
    </view>
  </block>

  <!-- 答题阶段 -->
  <block wx:elif="{{phase === 'quiz'}}">
    <view class="quiz-top">
      <text class="quiz-progress">第 {{quizIndex + 1}} / {{article.questions.length}} 
题</text>
      <text class="quiz-correct">✓ {{quizCorrect}}</text>
    </view>

    <view class="quiz-card">
      <view class="quiz-q">{{quizQuestion.q}}</view>
      <view class="options">
        <view
          wx:for="{{quizOptions}}"
          wx:key="letter"
          class="option {{quizAnswered && index === quizQuestion.answer ? 'is-correct'
 : ''}} {{quizAnswered && index === quizSelected && index !== quizQuestion.answer ? 'i
s-wrong' : ''}}"
          data-index="{{index}}"
          bindtap="choose"
        >
          <view class="option-letter">{{item.letter}}</view>
          <text class="option-text">{{item.text}}</text>
        </view>
      </view>

      <view wx:if="{{quizAnswered}}" class="explain">
        <view class="explain-hd">
          <text class="explain-emoji">{{quizSelected === quizQuestion.answer ? '🎉' : 
'📝'}}</text>
          <text class="explain-title">{{quizSelected === quizQuestion.answer ? '回答正
确' : '答案解析'}}</text>
        </view>
        <text class="explain-text">{{quizQuestion.explain}}</text>
      </view>
    </view>

    <view wx:if="{{quizAnswered}}" class="btn btn-primary btn-block" bindtap="next">
      {{quizIndex + 1 >= article.questions.length ? '查看成绩' : '下一题'}}
    </view>
  </block>

  <!-- 结算 -->
  <block wx:else>
    <view class="done-card">
      <view class="sticker sticker-green">阅读完成</view>
      <view class="done-score">{{result.accuracy}}<text class="done-unit">%</text></vi
ew>
      <view class="done-label">理解正确率</view>
      <view class="done-comment">{{result.comment}}</view>
      <view class="result-grid">
        <view class="result-cell">
          <text class="num">{{result.correct}}/{{result.total}}</text>
          <text class="result-label">答对题数</text>
        </view>
        <view class="result-cell">
          <text class="num">{{result.minutes}}</text>
          <text class="result-label">用时(分钟)</text>
        </view>
        <view class="result-cell">
          <text class="num">+{{result.exp}}</text>
          <text class="result-label">词力</text>
        </view>
      </view>
      <view class="done-tip">生词：{{article.keyWords[0].word}} · {{article.keyWords[1
].word}} · {{article.keyWords[2].word}}</view>
      <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block" bindtap="retry">再读一遍</view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/review/review.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 智能复习页
 * 从间隔复习队列中取到期单词，以四选一测验形式检测，
 * 答对升级熟练度，答错降级并写入错题病历本。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const wordsData = require('../../data/words.js')

Page({
  data: {
    bookId: 'all',
    queue: [],
    total: 0,
    index: 0,
    word: null,
    wordSize: 80,
    options: [],
    answer: 0,
    selected: -1,
    answered: false,
    isCorrect: false,
    progress: 0,
    phase: 'quiz',
    stats: { correct: 0, wrong: 0 },
    wrongList: [],
    result: null
  },

  onLoad(options) {
    const bookId = options && options.book ? options.book : 'all'
    this.setData({ bookId: bookId })
    this.buildQueue()
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
    this.minutesAdded = false
  },

  onUnload() {
    this.finishMinutes()
  },

  buildQueue() {
    let pool = []
    if (this.data.bookId === 'cet6') pool = wordsData.cet6
    else if (this.data.bookId === 'cet4') pool = wordsData.cet4
    else pool = wordsData.all

    let due = store.getDueWords(pool, 20)
    // 没有到期词时，用已学词做一次巩固练习
    if (!due.length) {
      due = pool
        .filter((w) => !!store.getRecord(w.id))
        .slice(0, 10)
    }

    if (!due.length) {
      this.setData({ phase: 'empty', queue: [], total: 0 })
      return
    }

    this.setData({
      phase: 'quiz',
      queue: due,
      total: due.length,
      index: 0,
      stats: { correct: 0, wrong: 0 },
      wrongList: [],
      result: null
    })
    this.loadQuestion(0)
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
    this.minutesAdded = false
  },

  loadQuestion(i) {
    const word = this.data.queue[i]
    if (!word) return
    const built = util.buildOptions(word, wordsData.all, 4)
    const options = built.options.map((o, i) => Object.assign({}, o, { letter: 'ABCD'[
i] }))
    const len = Math.max(4, word.word.length)
    this.setData({
      word: word,
      wordSize: Math.max(30, Math.min(80, Math.floor(620 / len))),
      options: options,
      answer: built.answer,
      selected: -1,
      answered: false,
      isCorrect: false,
      progress: Math.round(((i + 1) / this.data.queue.length) * 100)
    })
  },

  speak() {
    if (!this.data.word) return
    if (!this.data.word.phonetic) {
      wx.showToast({ title: '暂无音标', icon: 'none', duration: 900 })
      return
    }
    wx.showToast({ title: this.data.word.phonetic, icon: 'none', duration: 900 })
  },

  select(e) {
    if (this.data.answered) return
    const idx = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    const correct = idx === this.data.answer
    const word = this.data.word
    const stats = Object.assign({}, this.data.stats)
    const wrongList = this.data.wrongList.slice()

    if (correct) {
      stats.correct += 1
      store.vibrate('light')
    } else {
      stats.wrong += 1
      wrongList.push({ word: word.word, cn: word.cn })
      store.addWrong(word.id, '遗忘', { word: word.word, cn: word.cn })
    }

    store.submitWord(word, correct ? 2 : 0)
    this.setData({
      selected: idx,
      answered: true,
      isCorrect: correct,
      stats: stats,
      wrongList: wrongList
    })
  },

  next() {
    const nextIndex = this.data.index + 1
    if (nextIndex >= this.data.total) {
      this.finish()
      return
    }
    this.setData({ index: nextIndex })
    this.loadQuestion(nextIndex)
  },

  finish() {
    this.finishMinutes()
    const s = this.data.stats
    const total = s.correct + s.wrong
    const accuracy = total ? Math.round((s.correct / total) * 100) : 0
    this.setData({
      phase: 'done',
      result: {
        total: total,
        correct: s.correct,
        wrong: s.wrong,
        accuracy: accuracy,
        exp: total * 8,
        comment:
          accuracy >= 85
            ? '记忆很牢固，伙伴给你比了个心。'
            : accuracy >= 60
            ? '大部分都记得，错词再看一遍就更稳了。'
            : '错词已进入病历本，按处方复习效果更好。'
      }
    })
  },

  finishMinutes() {
    if (this.minutesAdded || !this.startedAt) return
    const minutes = (Date.now() - this.startedAt) / 60000
    if (minutes >= 0.5) store.addMinutes(minutes)
    this.minutesAdded = true
  },

  again() {
    this.buildQueue()
  },

  goStudy() {
    wx.redirectTo({ url: '/pages/study/study?book=' + (this.data.bookId === 'all' ? 'c
et4' : this.data.bookId) })
  },

  goWrongbook() {
    wx.redirectTo({ url: '/pages/wrongbook/wrongbook' })
  },

  goHome() {
    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
  },

  back() {
    const pages = getCurrentPages()
    if (pages.length > 1) wx.navigateBack()
    else wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
  }
})

<view class="page review-page">
  <!-- 答题 -->
  <block wx:if="{{phase === 'quiz'}}">
    <view class="quiz-top">
      <view class="close-btn" bindtap="back">✕</view>
      <view class="progress-wrap">
        <view class="bar bar-thin">
          <view class="bar-inner bar-green" style="width:{{progress}}%"></view>
        </view>
        <text class="progress-text">{{index + 1}} / {{total}}</text>
      </view>
      <view class="score-chip">✓ {{stats.correct}}</view>
    </view>

    <view class="quiz-card">
      <view class="quiz-tag">到期复习</view>
      <view class="quiz-word" style="font-size:{{wordSize}}rpx;">{{word.word}}</view>
      <view class="quiz-phonetic" wx:if="{{word.phonetic}}">
        <text>{{word.phonetic}}</text>
        <text class="quiz-speak" bindtap="speak">🔊</text>
      </view>
      <view class="quiz-ask">它的中文意思是？</view>
    </view>

    <view class="options">
      <view
        wx:for="{{options}}"
        wx:key="key"
        class="option {{answered && index === answer ? 'is-correct' : ''}} {{answered 
&& index === selected && index !== answer ? 'is-wrong' : ''}}"
        data-index="{{index}}"
        bindtap="select"
      >
        <view class="option-letter">{{item.letter}}</view>
        <text class="option-text">{{item.text}}</text>
        <text wx:if="{{answered && index === answer}}" class="option-mark">✓</text>
        <text wx:elif="{{answered && index === selected}}" class="option-mark">✕</text
>
      </view>
    </view>

    <!-- 解析 -->
    <view wx:if="{{answered}}" class="answer-box {{isCorrect ? 'is-right' : 'is-wrong'
}}">
      <view class="answer-hd">
        <text class="answer-emoji">{{isCorrect ? '🎉' : '🩺'}}</text>
        <text class="answer-title">{{isCorrect ? '答对啦' : '再记一遍'}}</text>
        <text class="answer-word">{{word.word}}</text>
      </view>
      <view class="answer-cn">{{word.pos}} {{word.cn}}</view>
      <view class="answer-en" wx:if="{{word.en}}">{{word.en}}</view>
      <view class="answer-tip" wx:if="{{word.tip}}">💡 {{word.tip}}</view>
    </view>

    <view wx:if="{{answered}}" class="btn btn-primary btn-block next-btn" bindtap="nex
t">
      {{index + 1 >= total ? '查看本次结果' : '下一题'}}
    </view>
  </block>

  <!-- 无到期词 -->
  <block wx:elif="{{phase === 'empty'}}">
    <empty-state emoji="🌤️" title="今天没有到期单词" desc="间隔复习会让记忆更牢，先
去学几个新词吧">
      <view class="row gap-16">
        <view class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" bindtap="goStudy">学新词</view>
        <view class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" bindtap="goHome">回小岛</view>
      </view>
    </empty-state>
  </block>

  <!-- 结算 -->
  <block wx:else>
    <view class="done-wrap">
      <view class="done-card">
        <view class="sticker sticker-green">复习完成</view>
        <view class="done-score">{{result.accuracy}}<text class="done-score-unit">%</t
ext></view>
        <view class="done-label">本次正确率</view>
        <view class="done-comment">{{result.comment}}</view>

        <view class="result-grid">
          <view class="result-cell">
            <text class="num">{{result.total}}</text>
            <text class="result-label">复习词数</text>
          </view>
          <view class="result-cell">
            <text class="num result-green">{{result.correct}}</text>
            <text class="result-label">答对</text>
          </view>
          <view class="result-cell">
            <text class="num result-red">{{result.wrong}}</text>
            <text class="result-label">答错</text>
          </view>
          <view class="result-cell">
            <text class="num">+{{result.exp}}</text>
            <text class="result-label">词力</text>
          </view>
        </view>

        <view wx:if="{{wrongList.length}}" class="wrong-box">
          <view class="wrong-title">本次错词</view>
          <view wx:for="{{wrongList}}" wx:key="word" class="wrong-row">
            <text class="wrong-word">{{item.word}}</text>
            <text class="wrong-cn">{{item.cn}}</text>
          </view>
        </view>

        <view class="done-btns">
          <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block" bindtap="again">再来一组</view>
          <view wx:if="{{wrongList.length}}" class="btn btn-ghost btn-block" bindtap="
goWrongbook">查看错题病历</view>
          <view class="btn btn-ghost btn-block" bindtap="goHome">回到小岛</view>
        </view>
      </view>
    </view>
  </block>
</view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/settings/settings.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 设置
 * 学习偏好、提醒时间与本地数据管理。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')

Page({
  data: {
    settings: null,
    storage: 0,
    version: '1.0.0'
  },

  onShow() {
    const state = store.getState()
    let storage = 0
    try {
      const info = wx.getStorageInfoSync()
      storage = info.currentSize || 0
    } catch (e) {}
    this.setData({
      settings: state.settings,
      storage: storage
    })
  },

  onSwitch(e) {
    const key = e.currentTarget.dataset.key
    const value = e.detail.value
    const state = store.getState()
    state.settings[key] = value
    store.save()
    this.setData({ settings: state.settings })
  },

  onReminder(e) {
    const value = e.detail.value
    const state = store.getState()
    state.settings.reminder = value
    store.save()
    this.setData({ settings: state.settings })
    wx.showToast({ title: '提醒时间已设为 ' + value, icon: 'none' })
  },

  clearData() {
    wx.showModal({
      title: '清除学习数据',
      content: '将删除本机所有学习记录、伙伴养成进度与错题本，且无法恢复。确定继续吗？
',
      confirmText: '清除',
      confirmColor: '#FF5C5C',
      success: (res) => {
        if (!res.confirm) return
        store.resetAll()
        wx.showToast({ title: '已恢复初始状态', icon: 'none' })
        setTimeout(() => {
          wx.reLaunch({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
        }, 600)
      }
    })
  },

  goAbout() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/about/about' })
  }
})

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/stats/stats.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 学习统计
 * 近 7 天柱状图、打卡热力图、熟练度分布与各模块完成度。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const wordsData = require('../../data/words.js')

Page({
  data: {
    stats: null,
    days: [],
    weeks: [],
    levels: [],
    levelLabels: ['未学', '1 级', '2 级', '3 级', '4 级', '5 级', '6 级'],
    modules: [],
    exams: [],
    fish: 0,
    bookProgress: []
  },

  onShow() {
    this.refresh()
  },

  refresh() {
    const state = store.getState()
    const stats = store.getStatsView()
    const heat = store.getHeatmap(8)
    const weeks = []
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      weeks.push({ key: 'w' + i, cells: heat.slice(i * 7, i * 7 + 7) })
    }

    const maxLevel = Math.max(1, stats.levels.reduce((m, n) => Math.max(m, n), 0))
    const levels = stats.levels.map((count, index) => ({
      index: index,
      count: count,
      percent: Math.round((count / maxLevel) * 100)
    }))

    const modules = [
      {
        name: '阅读闯关',
        emoji: '📖',
        done: state.readings.finished.length,
        total: 3,
        color: 'green'
      },
      {
        name: '影子跟读',
        emoji: '🎧',
        done: Object.keys(state.listenings.best).length,
        total: 3,
        color: 'blue'
      },
      {
        name: '情景对话',
        emoji: '💬',
        done: state.dialogues.finished.length,
        total: 4,
        color: 'pink'
      },
      {
        name: '词根拼图',
        emoji: '🧩',
        done: state.puzzle.cleared.length,
        total: 12,
        color: 'purple'
      }
    ].map((m) => Object.assign({}, m, { percent: util.percent(m.done, m.total) }))

    const exams = state.exam.history.slice(0, 3).map((e) => ({
      date: util.formatDate(e.at, 'MM-DD HH:mm'),
      score: e.score,
      correct: e.correct,
      total: e.total
    }))

    const bookProgress = wordsData.books.map((b) => {
      const p = store.getBookProgress(wordsData.getBook(b.id), b.id)
      return {
        id: b.id,
        name: b.name,
        emoji: b.emoji,
        learned: p.learned,
        total: p.total,
        percent: p.percent
      }
    })

    this.setData({
      stats: stats,
      days: stats.days,
      weeks: weeks,
      levels: levels,
      modules: modules,
      exams: exams,
      fish: state.wallet.fish,
      bookProgress: bookProgress
    })
  },

  goPlan() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/plan/plan' })
  },

  goWrongbook() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/wrongbook/wrongbook' })
  }
})

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/study/study.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 单词学习页
 * 流程：取新词队列 → 翻卡记忆 → 三档自评（不认识/模糊/认识）
 *       → 调用 store.submitWord 写入间隔复习计划 → 会话结算。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const audio = require('../../utils/audio.js')
const wordsData = require('../../data/words.js')

Page({
  data: {
    bookId: 'cet4',
    bookName: '',
    phase: 'learn',
    queue: [],
    total: 0,
    index: 0,
    word: null,
    wordSize: 92,
    backWordSize: 56,
    flipped: false,
    speaking: false,
    showPhonetic: true,
    progress: 0,
    session: {
      known: 0,
      fuzzy: 0,
      unknown: 0,
      exp: 0
    },
    result: null,
    pet: null,
    achievements: [],
    newPets: [],
    levelUp: false
  },

  onLoad(options) {
    const bookId = options && options.book === 'cet6' ? 'cet6' : 'cet4'
    const book = wordsData.books.filter((b) => b.id === bookId)[0]
    this.setData({
      bookId: bookId,
      bookName: book ? book.name : '四级全量词',
      showPhonetic: store.getState().settings.showPhonetic
    })
    this.startSession()
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
    this.minutesAdded = false
  },

  onUnload() {
    this.finishMinutes()
  },

  /** 根据单词长度计算字号，避免长词溢出卡片 */
  calcWordSize(word) {
    const len = Math.max(3, (word || '').length)
    return {
      wordSize: Math.max(26, Math.min(92, Math.floor(620 / len))),
      backWordSize: Math.max(24, Math.min(56, Math.floor(460 / len)))
    }
  },

  /** 组卷：优先取每日新词目标数量 */
  startSession() {
    const state = store.getState()
    const limit = Math.max(5, state.profile.dailyNew || 15)
    const queue = store.getNewWords(wordsData.getBook(this.data.bookId), limit)
    if (!queue.length) {
      this.setData({
        phase: 'empty',
        total: 0,
        queue: []
      })
      return
    }
    const size = this.calcWordSize(queue[0].word)
    this.setData({
      phase: 'learn',
      queue: queue,
      total: queue.length,
      index: 0,
      word: queue[0],
      wordSize: size.wordSize,
      backWordSize: size.backWordSize,
      flipped: false,
      progress: Math.round((1 / queue.length) * 100),
      session: { known: 0, fuzzy: 0, unknown: 0, exp: 0 },
      result: null,
      achievements: [],
      newPets: [],
      levelUp: false
    })
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
    this.minutesAdded = false
  },

  /** 朗读：示例工程未内置音频，展示音标与音波动画 */
  speak() {
    if (!this.data.word) return
    if (!this.data.word.phonetic) {
      wx.showToast({ title: '暂无音标', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    audio.playWord(this.data.word.word)
    this.setData({ speaking: true })
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.setData({ speaking: false })
    }, 1000)
    wx.showToast({
      title: this.data.word.phonetic,
      icon: 'none',
      duration: 1000
    })
  },

  flip() {
    this.setData({ flipped: !this.data.flipped })
  },

  /** 三档自评 */
  choose(e) {
    const quality = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.q)
    const word = this.data.word
    if (!word) return

    const res = store.submitWord(word, quality)
    if (quality === 0) {
      store.addWrong(word.id, '遗忘', { word: word.word, cn: word.cn })
    }
    if (quality === 2) {
      store.vibrate('light')
    }

    const session = Object.assign({}, this.data.session)
    if (quality === 2) session.known += 1
    else if (quality === 1) session.fuzzy += 1
    else session.unknown += 1
    session.exp += quality === 2 ? 10 : quality === 1 ? 7 : 5

    const achievements = this.data.achievements.slice()
    if (res.newAchievements && res.newAchievements.length) {
      res.newAchievements.forEach((a) => {
        achievements.push(a)
      })
    }

    const newPets = this.data.newPets.slice()
    if (res.newPets && res.newPets.length) {
      res.newPets.forEach((p) => {
        newPets.push(p)
      })
    }

    const nextIndex = this.data.index + 1
    const levelUp = this.data.levelUp || res.levelUp

    if (nextIndex >= this.data.queue.length) {
      this.setData({
        session: session,
        achievements: achievements,
        newPets: newPets,
        levelUp: levelUp
      })
      this.finish()
      return
    }

    const nextWord = this.data.queue[nextIndex]
    const size = this.calcWordSize(nextWord.word)
    this.setData({
      session: session,
      achievements: achievements,
      newPets: newPets,
      levelUp: levelUp,
      index: nextIndex,
      word: nextWord,
      wordSize: size.wordSize,
      backWordSize: size.backWordSize,
      flipped: false,
      progress: Math.round(((nextIndex + 1) / this.data.queue.length) * 100)
    })

    wx.showToast({
      title: quality === 2 ? '记住啦 +10 词力' : quality === 1 ? '模糊，明天再见' : '
已加入复习队列',
      icon: 'none',
      duration: 700
    })
  },

  /** 结算 */
  finish() {
    this.finishMinutes()
    const s = this.data.session
    const total = s.known + s.fuzzy + s.unknown
    const accuracy = total ? Math.round((s.known / total) * 100) : 0
    let comment = '今天的单词都喂给伙伴啦，继续保持！'
    if (accuracy >= 85) comment = '状态超好！伙伴开心得转了三圈。'
    else if (accuracy < 50) comment = '别急，明天复习一遍就会更牢。'

    this.setData({
      phase: 'done',
      pet: store.getPetView(),
      result: {
        total: total,
        known: s.known,
        fuzzy: s.fuzzy,
        unknown: s.unknown,
        exp: s.exp,
        accuracy: accuracy,
        comment: comment,
        minutes: Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - (this.startedAt || Date.now())) 
/ 60000))
      }
    })
  },

  finishMinutes() {
    if (this.minutesAdded || !this.startedAt) return
    const minutes = (Date.now() - this.startedAt) / 60000
    if (minutes >= 0.5) {
      store.addMinutes(minutes)
    }
    this.minutesAdded = true
  },

  again() {
    this.startSession()
  },

  goReview() {
    wx.redirectTo({ url: '/pages/review/review?book=' + this.data.bookId })
  },

  goHome() {
    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
  },

  back() {
    const pages = getCurrentPages()
    if (pages.length > 1) {
      wx.navigateBack()
    } else {
      wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
    }
  },

  noop() {}
})

 * ============================================================ */

  "navigationBarTitleText": "单词学习"
}

<view class="study-page">
  <!-- 学习进行中 -->
  <block wx:if="{{phase === 'learn'}}">
    <view class="study-top">
      <view class="close-btn" bindtap="back">✕</view>
      <view class="progress-wrap">
        <view class="bar bar-thin">
          <view class="bar-inner" style="width:{{progress}}%"></view>
        </view>
        <text class="progress-text">{{index + 1}} / {{total}}</text>
      </view>
      <view class="book-chip">{{bookName}}</view>
    </view>

    <view class="card-stage">
      <!-- 卡片正面 -->
      <view wx:if="{{!flipped}}" class="word-card anim-pop" bindtap="flip">
        <view class="card-tape"></view>
        <view class="word-tag">新词</view>
        <view class="word-big" style="font-size:{{wordSize}}rpx;">{{word.word}}</view>
        <view class="phonetic-row" wx:if="{{showPhonetic}}">
          <view class="wave {{speaking ? 'wave-play' : ''}}">
            <view class="wave-item"></view>
            <view class="wave-item"></view>
            <view class="wave-item"></view>
            <view class="wave-item"></view>
            <view class="wave-item"></view>
          </view>
          <text class="phonetic-text">{{word.phonetic}}</text>
          <view class="speak-btn" catchtap="speak">🔊</view>
        </view>
        <view class="word-pos" wx:if="{{word.pos}}">{{word.pos}}</view>
        <view class="flip-hint">
          <text class="flip-hand">👆</text>
          <text>点一下卡片，看看它的意思</text>
        </view>
        <view class="word-deco word-deco-1">✦</view>
        <view class="word-deco word-deco-2">✧</view>
      </view>

      <!-- 卡片背面 -->
      <view wx:else class="word-card word-card-back anim-pop" bindtap="flip">
        <view class="back-hd">
          <view>
            <text class="back-word" style="font-size:{{backWordSize}}rpx;">{{word.word
}}</text>
            <text class="back-phonetic" wx:if="{{showPhonetic}}">{{word.phonetic}}</te
xt>
          </view>
          <text class="tag tag-orange" wx:if="{{word.pos}}">{{word.pos}}</text>
        </view>
        <view class="back-cn">{{word.cn}}</view>

          <text class="example-en">{{word.en}}</text>
          <text class="example-cn">{{word.cnSent}}</text>
        </view>
        <view class="example-box bare" wx:else>
          <text class="bare-title">先记住它</text>
          <text class="example-cn">该词暂无例句：跟着音标读三遍，再复述一遍中文释义。<
/text>
        </view>

        <view class="root-box" wx:if="{{word.roots.length}}">
          <view wx:for="{{word.roots}}" wx:key="t" class="root-piece">
            <text class="piece-text">{{item.t}}</text>
            <text class="piece-mean">{{item.m}}</text>
          </view>
        </view>

        <view class="tip-box" wx:if="{{word.tip}}">
          <text class="tip-icon">💡</text>
          <text class="tip-text">{{word.tip}}</text>
        </view>
        <view class="back-hint">再点一下可以翻回去</view>
      </view>
    </view>

    <!-- 操作区 -->
    <view wx:if="{{!flipped}}" class="action-area">
      <view class="btn btn-primary btn-block" bindtap="flip">显示释义</view>
      <text class="action-tip">先在心里回忆一遍，再翻开效果更好</text>
    </view>
    <view wx:else class="action-area">
      <view class="judge-row">
        <view class="judge judge-unknown" data-q="0" bindtap="choose">
          <text class="judge-emoji">😵</text>
          <text class="judge-name">不认识</text>
          <text class="judge-desc">10 分钟后</text>
        </view>
        <view class="judge judge-fuzzy" data-q="1" bindtap="choose">
          <text class="judge-emoji">🤔</text>
          <text class="judge-name">有点模糊</text>
          <text class="judge-desc">明天再见</text>
        </view>
        <view class="judge judge-known" data-q="2" bindtap="choose">
          <text class="judge-emoji">😎</text>
          <text class="judge-name">认识</text>
          <text class="judge-desc">间隔拉长</text>
        </view>
      </view>
    </view>
  </block>

  <!-- 没有新词 -->
  <block wx:elif="{{phase === 'empty'}}">
    <view class="done-wrap">
      <empty-state emoji="🌿" title="这本词书的新词学完啦" desc="去复习巩固，或者切换
到另一本词书">
        <view class="row gap-16">
          <view class="btn btn-green btn-sm" bindtap="goReview">去复习</view>
          <view class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" bindtap="goHome">回小岛</view>
        </view>
      </empty-state>
    </view>
  </block>

  <!-- 会话结算 -->
  <block wx:else>
    <view class="done-wrap">
      <view class="done-card">
        <view class="done-spark done-spark-1">✦</view>
        <view class="done-spark done-spark-2">✧</view>

        <pet-avatar size="200" species="{{pet.species}}" color="{{pet.color}}" accent=

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/writing/writing.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 写作工坊
 * 写作：命题作文 + 实时字数 + 四维诊断（篇幅、句式、衔接、词汇）
 * 翻译：段落翻译 + 采分点 + 参考译文对照
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const writingData = require('../../data/writing.js')

Page({
  data: {
    tab: 'essay',
    essays: writingData.essays,
    translations: writingData.translations,
    essayIndex: 0,
    essay: null,
    translationIndex: 0,
    translation: null,
    text: '',
    wordCount: 0,
    result: null,
    showSample: false,
    showRef: false,
    transText: '',
    minutes: 0,
    diagnosing: false
  },

  onLoad() {
    this.setData({
      essay: writingData.essays[0],
      translation: writingData.translations[0]
    })
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
    this.timer = setInterval(() => {
      this.setData({ minutes: Math.floor((Date.now() - this.startedAt) / 60000) })
    }, 10000)
  },

  onUnload() {
    if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer)
    const minutes = (Date.now() - (this.startedAt || Date.now())) / 60000
    if (minutes >= 1) store.addMinutes(minutes)
  },

  switchTab(e) {
    const tab = e.currentTarget.dataset.tab
    this.setData({ tab: tab })
  },

  selectEssay(e) {
    const index = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    this.setData({
      essayIndex: index,
      essay: this.data.essays[index],
      text: '',
      wordCount: 0,
      result: null,
      showSample: false
    })
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
  },

  selectTranslation(e) {
    const index = Number(e.currentTarget.dataset.index)
    this.setData({
      translationIndex: index,
      translation: this.data.translations[index],
      transText: '',
      showRef: false
    })
  },

  onInput(e) {
    const text = e.detail.value
    const words = text.trim() ? text.trim().split(/\s+/).filter((w) => /[a-zA-Z]/.test
(w)).length : 0
    this.setData({ text: text, wordCount: words })
  },

  onTransInput(e) {
    this.setData({ transText: e.detail.value })
  },

  toggleSample() {
    this.setData({ showSample: !this.data.showSample })
  },

  toggleRef() {
    this.setData({ showRef: !this.data.showRef })
  },

  /** 本地启发式诊断，不依赖网络 */
  diagnose() {
    if (this.data.wordCount < 20) {
      wx.showToast({ title: '至少写 20 个词再诊断哦', icon: 'none' })
      return
    }
    this.setData({ diagnosing: true })
    const result = util.analyzeWriting(this.data.text)
    const minutes = Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - this.startedAt) / 60000))
    store.saveWriting(result.score)
    store.logActivity({
      exp: 15 + Math.round(result.score / 10),
      fish: 6,
      minutes: minutes,
      correct: 1
    })
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.setData({ result: result, diagnosing: false })
      wx.showToast({ title: '诊断完成', icon: 'none' })
    }, 500)
  },

  resetText() {
    this.setData({ text: '', wordCount: 0, result: null })
  },

  goHome() {
    wx.switchTab({ url: '/pages/index/index' })
  }
})

    </scroll-view>

        </view>
      </view>

xt>

}">

          <view class="result-grade">{{result.grade}}</view>
          <view class="result-sub">{{result.wordCount}} 词 · {{result.sentenceCount}} 
句 · 平均 {{result.avgLen}} 词/句</view>
          <view class="result-tags">
            <text class="tag tag-green">{{result.uniqueCount}} 个不同单词</text>
            <text class="tag tag-blue">{{result.links.length}} 处衔接</text>
          </view>
        </view>
      </view>

      <view class="dimension">
        <view class="dim-row">
          <text class="dim-name">篇幅</text>
          <view class="bar bar-thin flex1"><view class="bar-inner" style="width:{{resu
lt.wordCount >= 120 ? 100 : result.wordCount / 1.2}}%"></view></view>
        </view>
        <view class="dim-row">
          <text class="dim-name">句式</text>
          <view class="bar bar-thin flex1 bar-blue"><view class="bar-inner" style="wid
th:{{result.sentenceCount >= 8 ? 100 : result.sentenceCount * 12}}%"></view></view>
        </view>
        <view class="dim-row">
          <text class="dim-name">衔接</text>
          <view class="bar bar-thin flex1 bar-green"><view class="bar-inner" style="wi
dth:{{result.links.length * 18 > 100 ? 100 : result.links.length * 18}}%"></view></vie
w>
        </view>
        <view class="dim-row">
          <text class="dim-name">词汇</text>
          <view class="bar bar-thin flex1 bar-pink"><view class="bar-inner" style="wid
th:{{result.uniqueRate}}%"></view></view>
        </view>
      </view>

      <view class="advice-box">

        <view wx:for="{{result.advice}}" wx:key="*this" class="advice-item">

        </view>
      </view>

      <view class="sample-toggle" bindtap="toggleSample">

      </view>
      <view wx:if="{{showSample}}" class="sample">{{essay.sample}}</view>
    </view>

    <view wx:if="{{!result}}" class="sample-toggle plain" bindtap="toggleSample">
      {{showSample ? '收起参考范文 ▲' : '先看看参考范文 ▼'}}
    </view>
    <view wx:if="{{!result && showSample}}" class="sample">{{essay.sample}}</view>
  </block>

  <!-- 翻译 -->
  <block wx:else>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：pages/wrongbook/wrongbook.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 错题病历本
 * 按错因归类，自动生成复习处方；确认掌握后可将病历标记为已治愈。
 */
const store = require('../../utils/store.js')
const util = require('../../utils/util.js')
const wordsData = require('../../data/words.js')

const REASON_COLOR = {
  形近混淆: 'orange',
  音近听错: 'blue',
  遗忘: 'red',
  语境误解: 'purple'
}

Page({
  data: {
    list: [],
    view: [],
    filter: 'all',
    filters: [],
    stats: { total: 0, byReason: {} },
    expanded: '',
    resolvedCount: 0
  },

  onShow() {
    this.refresh()
  },

  refresh() {
    const raw = store.getWrongList()
    const stats = store.getWrongStats()
    const list = raw
      .filter((w) => !w.resolved)
      .map((w) => {
        const word = wordsData.getById(w.wordId)
        return {
          id: w.id,
          wordId: w.wordId,
          word: w.word || (word ? word.word : '未知单词'),
          phonetic: word ? word.phonetic : '',
          cn: w.cn || (word ? word.cn : ''),
          pos: word ? word.pos : '',
          en: word ? word.en : '',
          tip: word ? word.tip : '',
          reason: w.reason,
          color: REASON_COLOR[w.reason] || 'red',
          count: w.count,
          dateText: util.formatDate(w.at, 'MM月DD日'),
          prescription: util.prescription(w.reason)
        }
      })

    const filters = [
      { key: 'all', label: '全部', count: stats.total }
    ].concat(
      store.REASONS.map((r) => ({
        key: r,
        label: r,
        count: stats.byReason[r] || 0
      }))
    )

    this.setData({
      list: list,
      stats: stats,
      filters: filters,
      resolvedCount: raw.filter((w) => w.resolved).length
    })
    this.applyFilter(this.data.filter)
  },

  applyFilter(key) {
    const view = this.data.list.filter((w) => key === 'all' || w.reason === key)
    this.setData({ filter: key, view: view })
  },

  onFilter(e) {
    this.applyFilter(e.currentTarget.dataset.key)
  },

  toggle(e) {
    const id = e.currentTarget.dataset.id
    this.setData({ expanded: this.data.expanded === id ? '' : id })
  },

  resolve(e) {
    const wordId = e.currentTarget.dataset.wordid
    store.resolveWrong(wordId)
    wx.showToast({ title: '已标记为掌握', icon: 'none' })
    this.refresh()
  },

  goReview() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/review/review' })
  },

  goStudy() {
    wx.navigateTo({ url: '/pages/study/study' })
  }
})

      <view
        wx:for="{{filters}}"
        wx:key="key"
        wx:if="{{index > 0}}"
        class="reason-item"
      >
        <text class="reason-name">{{item.label}}</text>
        <text class="reason-count">{{item.count}}</text>
      </view>
    </view>
    <view class="reason-bar">
      <view
        wx:for="{{filters}}"
        wx:key="key"
        wx:if="{{index > 0 && item.count > 0}}"
        class="reason-seg seg-{{index}}"
        style="width:{{item.count / (stats.total || 1) * 100}}%"
      ></view>
    </view>
  </view>

  <!-- 筛选 -->
  <scroll-view scroll-x class="filter-scroll" enhanced show-scrollbar="{{false}}">
    <view class="filter-row">
      <view
        wx:for="{{filters}}"
        wx:key="key"
        class="filter-chip {{filter === item.key ? 'is-active' : ''}}"
        data-key="{{item.key}}"
        bindtap="onFilter"
      >{{item.label}} {{item.count}}</view>
    </view>
  </scroll-view>

  <!-- 病历列表 -->

      <view class="wrong-hd">
        <view class="flex1">

            <text class="wrong-phonetic">{{item.phonetic}}</text>
          </view>
          <text class="wrong-cn">{{item.pos}} {{item.cn}}</text>
        </view>
        <text class="reason-tag bg-{{item.color}}">{{item.reason}}</text>
      </view>

      <view class="wrong-meta">
        <text>答错 {{item.count}} 次</text>
        <text>·</text>
        <text>{{item.dateText}} 记录</text>
      </view>

      <view wx:if="{{expanded === item.id}}" class="prescription">
        <view class="prescription-hd">💊 复习处方</view>
        <text class="prescription-text">{{item.prescription}}</text>
        <view class="prescription-example" wx:if="{{item.en}}">
          <text class="pe-label">回到语境</text>
          <text class="pe-text">{{item.en}}</text>
        </view>
        <view class="prescription-tip" wx:if="{{item.tip}}">记忆提示：{{item.tip}}</vi
ew>
      </view>

      <view class="wrong-actions">
        <view class="wrong-toggle" data-id="{{item.id}}" bindtap="toggle">
          {{expanded === item.id ? '收起处方 ▲' : '查看处方 ▼'}}
        </view>
        <view class="btn btn-xs btn-green" data-wordid="{{item.wordId}}" bindtap="reso
lve">已掌握</view>
      </view>
    </view>
  </view>

  <empty-state
    wx:else
    emoji="🌿"
    title="病历本空空的"
    desc="答错或标记不认识的单词会自动记录在这里"
  >

      <view class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" bindtap="goReview">去复习</view>
    </view>
  </empty-state>

  <view class="wrong-footer">
    错题不是失败，是还没点亮的星星。按处方复习，错词会变成熟词。
  </view>
</view>

/* ============================================================
 * 文件：utils/audio.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 词灵岛 · 发音模块
 * 小程序不内置英文 TTS，因此约定：把 mp3 放入 assets/audio/ 目录，
 * 文件名与单词一致（小写），即可自动播放。
 * 未内置音频时不报错，由页面展示音标与音波动画作为提示。
 */
let ctx = null
let currentWord = ''
const appStore = require('./store.js')

function getContext() {
  if (ctx) return ctx
  try {
    ctx = wx.createInnerAudioContext()
    ctx.obeyMuteSwitch = false
    ctx.volume = 1
  } catch (e) {
    ctx = null
  }
  return ctx
}

function hasAudio(word) {
  // 当前示例工程未内置音频文件，返回 false 走音标提示分支。
  // 接入真实音频后：return true
  return !!(word && false)
}

/**
 * 播放单词发音
 * @param {String} word 单词
 * @param {Function} onState 播放状态回调 (playing:Boolean)
 */
function playWord(word, onState) {
  const w = (word || '').toLowerCase()
  const settings = appStore.getState().settings
  if (!settings.sound || !w || !hasAudio(w)) {
    if (typeof onState === 'function') onState(false)
    return false
  }
  const audio = getContext()
  if (!audio) return false
  try {
    if (currentWord !== w) {
      audio.stop()
      currentWord = w
    }
    audio.src = '/assets/audio/' + w + '.mp3'
    audio.play()
    if (typeof onState === 'function') onState(true)
    audio.onEnded(() => {
      if (typeof onState === 'function') onState(false)
    })
    audio.onError(() => {
      if (typeof onState === 'function') onState(false)
    })
    return true
  } catch (e) {
    if (typeof onState === 'function') onState(false)
    return false
  }
}

function stop() {
  if (ctx) {
    try {
      ctx.stop()
    } catch (e) {}
  }
}

module.exports = {
  playWord: playWord,
  stop: stop,
  hasAudio: hasAudio
}


/* ============================================================
 * 文件：utils/plan.js
 * 软件：词灵岛四六级英语学习软件 V1.0.0
 * ============================================================ */
/**
 * 词灵岛 · 备考计划生成器
 * 根据考试日期、目标分数与当前词汇量，生成逐日学习安排。
 */
const util = require('./util.js')

/**
 * @param {Object} profile { examDate, dailyNew, targetScore }
 * @param {Object} progress { total, learned, mastered }
 * @returns {Object} { days, totalDays, todayTask, schedule }
 */
function buildPlan(profile, progress) {
  const totalDays = Math.max(1, util.daysUntil(profile.examDate))
  const dailyNew = Math.max(5, Number(profile.dailyNew) || 15)
  const remain = Math.max(0, progress.total - progress.learned)
  const reviewPerDay = Math.min(60, Math.max(15, Math.round((progress.learned || 0) * 
0.25)))
  const needDays = Math.ceil(remain / dailyNew)

  const schedule = []
  const showDays = Math.min(totalDays, 14)
  for (let i = 0; i < showDays; i++) {
    const date = new Date(Date.now() + i * 86400000)
    const isToday = i === 0
    const newCount = remain > 0 ? Math.min(dailyNew, Math.max(0, remain - i * dailyNew
)) : 0
    let stage = '基础积累'
    if (totalDays - i <= 14) stage = '冲刺模考'
    else if (totalDays - i <= 30) stage = '真题强化'
    else if (i > 0 && i % 7 === 0) stage = '周复盘'
    schedule.push({
      index: i + 1,
      date: util.formatDate(date, 'MM月DD日'),
      week: util.weekdayCN(date),
      isToday: isToday,
      newWords: newCount,
      reviewWords: Math.min(reviewPerDay, progress.learned || 0),
      stage: stage,
      done: false
    })
  }

  // 按剩余天数均摊，给出每日节奏建议
  let pace = '节奏轻松'
  if (totalDays > 0 && remain / totalDays > 25) pace = '节奏紧张，建议提高每日新词'
  else if (totalDays > 0 && remain / totalDays > 15) pace = '节奏适中，保持每日打卡'

  return {
    totalDays: totalDays,
    dailyNew: dailyNew,
    remain: remain,
    reviewPerDay: reviewPerDay,
    needDays: needDays,
    onTrack: needDays <= totalDays,
    pace: pace,
    schedule: schedule
  }
}

/** 根据剩余天数给出三个阶段的建议 */
function getPhases(profile) {
  const days = Math.max(1, util.daysUntil(profile.examDate))
  const phases = [
    {
      key: 'base',
      name: '基础积累',
      emoji: '🌱',
      desc: '主攻高频词，建立第一轮记忆',
      days: Math.max(0, days - 30),
      color: 'green'
    },
    {
      key: 'boost',
      name: '真题强化',
      emoji: '🔥',
      desc: '真题精练，查漏补缺',
      days: Math.min(30, Math.max(0, days - 14)),
      color: 'orange'
    },
    {
      key: 'sprint',
      name: '冲刺模考',
      emoji: '🚀',
      desc: '全真模考，稳定手感',
      days: Math.min(14, days),
      color: 'pink'
    }
  ]
  return phases
}

module.exports = {
  buildPlan: buildPlan,
  getPhases: getPhases
}

 * 词灵岛 · 本地数据仓库

 * 采用单一 JSON 快照持久化（wx.setStorageSync），页面只通过本模块
 * 读写学习数据，保证「单词记录、复习计划、词灵成长、知识花园、
 * 成就任务」之间的状态一致。
 *
 * 复习算法：改进版 Leitner / SM-2 简化模型
 *   熟练度 0-6 级，对应下次复习间隔 [10分钟, 1, 2, 4, 7, 15, 30] 天
 *   认识 → 升级 + 间隔后移；模糊 → 保持等级，次日再见；
 *   不认识 → 降级 + 10 分钟后重现。
 */
const chaptersData = require('../data/chapters.js')
const achievementsData = require('../data/achievements.js')

const MINUTE = 60 * 1000

const AGAIN_GAP = 10 * MINUTE

const FLOWER_COLORS = ['#FF86AE', '#FFD166', '#7BDCB5', '#7FB8FF', '#C3A6FF', '#FF9F1C
']

// 用于「词汇结构驱动进化」的词缀表（不含连字符，便于直接匹配单词）

]
const SUFFIXES = [
  'tion', 'sion', 'ment', 'ness', 'ity', 'ance', 'ence', 'ship', 'hood', 'ism',
  'ist', 'er', 'or', 'able', 'ible', 'ous', 'ive', 'al', 'ic', 'ly',
  'ful', 'less', 'ify', 'ize', 'ise', 'ate', 'en', 'age', 'ary', 'ory',
  'ent', 'ant', 'ure', 'y'
]

let vocabCache = null

const TASK_POOL = [
  { id: 'new', emoji: '📖', title: '学习 {n} 个新词', metric: 'newWords', target: 10, 
reward: 20 },

 reward: 15 }
